经许可重印自参考文献[52], [39] &amp; [55]。版权所有 © 2018 和 2022 美国化学会。版权所有 © 2019 皇家化学学会。

The fitted GPRI parameters to the reference DFT data in Fig. 6(f) for WTe2 are: σW1
Te1 = σTe1
W1 = 0.01a; σW1
Te2 = σTe2
W1 = 0.16a; σW2
Te1 = σTe1
W1 = 0.07a; σW2
Te2 = σTe2
W2 = 0.18a; α = 0.77Å−1, where a = 6.26 Å is the interlayer lattice constant along the x direction.
拟合图6(f)中WTe2的参考DFT数据的GPRI参数为：σ_</span>W1 Te1 = σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_Te1 W1 = 0.01a; σ_</span>W1 Te2 = σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_Te2 W1 = 0.16a; σ_</span>W2 Te1 = σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_Te1 W1 = 0.07a; σ_</span>W2 Te2 = σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_Te2 W2 = 0.18a; α = 0.77 Å^−1，其中a = 6.26 Å是沿x方向的层间晶格常数。

It was pointed out that the Gaussian widths for Te2-W1(2) are larger than those for Te1-W1(2), consistent with the fact that Te2 atoms possess a larger effective charge than the Te1 atoms.
研究表明，Te2-W1(2)的高斯宽度大于Te1-W1(2)的，这与Te2原子比Te1原子具有更大有效电荷的事实一致。

Fig. 6(g) shows the local polarization registry index (LPRI) profile for the 2° twisted WTe2 in stacking mode I configuration.
图6(g)显示了在堆垛模式I构型下，2°转角WTe2的局部极化对准指数分布图。

As visible in 6(g), there are two types of domain walls: one type at x = 0 (18) nm, and the other type at x = 9 (27) nm.
如图6(g)所示，存在两种类型的畴壁：一种位于x = 0 (18) nm处，另一种位于x = 9 (27) nm处。

The first (broader) type belongs to the low registry (high energy) stacking order, while the second (narrower) type represents the intermediate stacking order separating the stacking modes I and II.
第一种（较宽）类型属于低对准度（高能量）堆垛序，而第二种（较窄）类型代表分隔堆垛模式I和II的中间堆垛序。

# 2.2.2 Spin Field Effect Transistor (Spin FET)
# 2.2.2 自旋场效应晶体管

An investigation by Liu et al. [55] reported the spin texture reversal with sliding FE polarization in the WTe2 bilayer, similar to what was first predicted and later observed in FE Rashba semiconductor bulk GeTe.[56–58]
Liu等人[55]的一项研究报告了WTe2双层中自旋织构随滑动铁电极化的翻转，这类似于首次预测并随后在铁电Rashba半导体块体GeTe中观察到的现象。[56–58]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Rashba semiconductor**</span>: 拉什巴半导体，一类由于结构反演不对称性，在动量空间中产生自旋劈裂的半导体材料。

Based on the original idea of Datta and Das spin FET [59], a design of spin FET was proposed with injector and analyzer having in-plane and out-of-plane magnetic anisotropies, respectively.[56]
基于Datta和Das自旋场效应管的原始构想[59]，提出了一种自旋场效应管的设计，其注入器和分析器分别具有面内和面外磁各向异性。[56]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Spin FET**</span>: 自旋场效应晶体管，一种利用电子自旋而非电荷进行信息处理和传输的场效应晶体管。

The spin-texture dependence on FE polarization allows the modulation of current density at the analyzer.
自旋织构对铁电极化的依赖性允许在分析器处调制电流密度。

For GeTe spin FET, with channel length (l) of 60 Å (reasonably within spin diffusion length) and Rashba momentum offset (kR) of 0.013 Å-1, phase difference [∆θ = 2kRl] of ±π/2 is accumulated by the carriers in the polarization state ±P as reaching the analyzer with out-of-plane magnetic anisotropy.
对于GeTe自旋场效应管，沟道长度(l)为60 Å（合理地在自旋扩散长度内），拉什巴动量偏移(kR)为0.013 Å^-1，当载流子到达具有面外磁各向异性的分析器时，在±P极化态下会积累±π/2的相位差 [∆θ = 2k_</span>Rl]。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Spin diffusion length**</span>: 自旋扩散长度，电子在散射（翻转）其自旋之前在材料中移动的平均距离。

Thus, the current arrives with spin parallel (antiparallel) to the majority carriers of the analyzer, as the FE polarization of GeTe is positive (negative).
因此，当GeTe的铁电极化为正（负）时，电流到达分析器时，其自旋方向与分析器的多数载流子平行（反平行）。

Similarly, the design of spin FET will work for the WTe2 bilayer with polarization resulting from sliding ferroelectricity and the spin texture dependence on it (refer to Fig. 6 (h-k)).
类似地，自旋场效应管的设计也适用于WTe2双层，其极化来自滑动铁电性，且自旋织构依赖于它（参见图6(h-k)）。

It has been observed previously that the resistance mismatch between the channel material and ferromagnetic (FM) electrode could be as large as 104.
此前已观察到，沟道材料与铁磁电极之间的电阻失配可能高达10^4。

This leads to poor spin-injection efficiency, one of the challenges faced in the spin FET operation.[60]
这导致自旋注入效率低下，是自旋场效应管运行中面临的挑战之一。[60]

A report by Fei et al., via conductivity measurement, demonstrated that though the WTe2 bilayer is an insulator with electrical conductivity of 200 μS, n-layered WTe2 (n ≥ 3) is metallic with trilayer conductivity of 2000 μS at 4K temperature.[51]
Fei等人通过电导率测量的一份报告表明，尽管WTe2双层是绝缘体，电导率为200 μS，但n层WTe2 (n ≥ 3) 是金属性的，在4K温度下三层电导率为2000 μS。[51]

Using this inference, Liu et al. proposed a design for spin FET, utilizing the layered WTe2 for better impedance matching (refer to Fig. 6 (k)).
利用这一推论，Liu等人提出了一种自旋场效应管的设计，利用层状WTe2来实现更好的阻抗匹配（参见图6(k)）。

In the spin FET device design, the electrodes made using WTe2 (n ≥ 3) system, covered with FM metal and WTe2 bilayer as channel material, is predicted to possess better impedance matching.
在自旋场效应管器件设计中，使用WTe2 (n ≥ 3) 体系制成的电极，覆盖铁磁金属，并以WTe2双层作为沟道材料，预计具有更好的阻抗匹配。

Liu et al. investigated the ferroelectric to paraelectric phase transition by applying biaxial strain and its effect on barrier energy and polarization.[55]
Liu等人研究了通过施加双轴应变引起的铁电-顺电相变及其对势垒能量和极化的影响。[55]

<span class="hljs-section"># 2.3 BX (X= P, As, Sb), YN (Y= Al, Ga, In), and ZC (Z= Si, Ge, Sn) bilayers</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 2.3 BX (X= P, As, Sb), YN (Y= Al, Ga, In), 和 ZC (Z= Si, Ge, Sn) 双层</span>

Wang et al. discussed sliding ferroelectricity in several binary compounds with honeycomb structure.[61]
Wang等人讨论了具有蜂窝结构的几种二元化合物中的滑动铁电性。[61]

With a structure similar to BN (discussed in section 2.1), the potential of group III-V and group IV binary compounds as sliding ferroelectric was explored.
利用与BN（在第2.1节中讨论过）类似的结构，探索了III-V族和IV族二元化合物作为滑动铁电体的潜力。

It was shown that only the AB and AC stackings possess opposite polarization with degenerate energy.
研究表明，只有AB和AC堆垛具有能量简并、方向相反的极化。

Fig. 7(a) &amp; (b) display the side views of three of the nine studied structures in the AC stacking and the corresponding energy barrier and polarization along the switching pathway AC→AB.
图7(a)和(b)显示了所研究的九个结构中三个在AC堆垛下的侧视图，以及沿翻转路径AC→AB的能量势垒和极化。

The values of polarization and energy barrier are provided in Table 1.
极化和能量势垒的值在表1中提供。

Of the nine, only two BSb and InN systems have buckled structures; others possess planar structures.
在九个体系中，只有BSb和InN两个体系具有褶皱结构；其他体系具有平面结构。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Buckled structures**</span>: 褶皱结构，指二维材料表面并非完全平坦，而是存在原子高度的起伏结构。

The buckled structure was noted to accompany the larger polarization and energy barrier.
注意到褶皱结构伴随着更大的极化和能量势垒。

Fig. 7: (a) Side views of BP, GaN, and GeC bilayers in the AC stacking configuration.
图7: (a) AC堆垛构型下BP、GaN和GeC双层的侧视图。

(b) Energy barrier and polarization along the AC → AB stacking switching pathway, calculated using the NEB method.
(b) 使用NEB方法计算的沿AC → AB堆垛翻转路径的能量势垒和极化。

(c) Correlation between the optimal interlayer distance and the anion-to-cation electronegativity ratio (Ea/Ec).
(c) 最佳层间距与阴离子-阳离子电负性比(Ea/Ec)之间的相关性。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Electronegativity**</span>: 电负性，原子在化合物中吸引电子能力的一种量度。

(d) Polarization as a function of the system's effective distance deff.
(d) 极化作为体系有效距离d<span class="hljs-emphasis">_eff的函数。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Effective distance (deff)**</span>: 有效距离，定义为最佳层间距与组成原子间共价键长之差，用于更好地描述极化趋势。

(e) Relationship between polarization and the electronegativity ratio normalized by the effective distance.
(e) 极化与通过有效距离归一化的电负性比之间的关系。

(f) Relative stability of configurations, shown by the energy difference between the AA′ (or AC′ for BP and BAs) configuration and the AC configuration.
(f) 构型的相对稳定性，通过AA′（或BP和BAs的AC′）构型与AC构型之间的能量差来显示。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [61]. Copyright © 2023 American Physical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[61]。版权所有 © 2023 美国物理学会。

Next, the effect of interlayer distance was discussed by a point charge model.
接下来，通过点电荷模型讨论了层间距的影响。

The electric dipole moment per unit cell (p) was assessed by computing the induced dipole moment in the upper layer due to the electric field generated by the ions in the lower layer.
通过计算下层离子产生的电场在上层诱导的偶极矩，评估了每个晶胞的电偶极矩(p)。

It was expressed as:
它被表示为：

p ∝ C0
d2−R02 − CA
√L2A+(d−RA)2
+ CC
√L2C+(d−RC)2
(eq. 14)
p ∝ C_</span>0 / (d^2 - R<span class="hljs-emphasis">_0^2) - C_</span>A / √(L<span class="hljs-emphasis">_A^2 + (d - R_</span>A)^2) + C<span class="hljs-emphasis">_C / √(L_</span>C^2 + (d - R<span class="hljs-emphasis">_C)^2) (eq. 14)

where d is the interlayer distance, the parameters with subscript A (C) are anion (cation) related material parameters.
其中d是层间距，带下标A (C)的参数是与阴离子（阳离子）相关的材料参数。

The first term is due to cations just below the anions in the above layer, the second term is from anions of the lower layer, and the third term arises from the remaining cations of the lower layer.
第一项源于紧邻上层阴离子下方的阳离子，第二项来自下层的阴离子，第三项来自下层的剩余阳离子。

It was reported that the model fits well for the equilibrium distance and beyond (not shown in Fig. 7), with further reduction in interlayer distance leading to other interactions, aside from the van der Waals interaction becoming dominant.
据报道，该模型在平衡距离及更远距离上拟合良好（图7中未显示），随着层间距进一步减小，除了范德华相互作用外，其他相互作用开始占主导地位。

The inference was made that the smaller the interlayer distance, the higher the polarization.
推断出层间距越小，极化越高。

Further, to explain the polarization with the material trend, the electronegativity ratio was introduced.
此外，为了解释极化随材料的变化趋势，引入了电负性比。

It was seen that the greater the electronegativity ratio, the larger the polarization of the system.
可以看出，电负性比越大，体系的极化就越大。

Also, the correlation between optimal interlayer distance and electronegativity ratio was indicated (see Fig. 7(c)).
还指出了最佳层间距和电负性比之间的相关性（见图7(c)）。

Though the polarization variation with the optimal interlayer distance showed some trend, it was proposed that the effective distance (deff), defined as the difference between optimal interlayer distance and the covalent bond length between the two constituent atoms, the trend with the polarization in the system is more promising (refer to Fig. 7(d)).
尽管极化随最佳层间距的变化显示出某种趋势，但提出有效距离（定义为最佳层间距与两个组成原子之间共价键长的差值）与体系极化的趋势更具前景（参见图7(d)）。

With both electronegativity ratio and effective distance influencing the polarization, a plot of polarization versus electronegativity ratio divided by effective distance was demonstrated (Fig. 7(e)).
由于电负性比和有效距离都影响极化，展示了极化与电负性比除以有效距离的关系图（图7(e)）。

Next, the relative stability of all possible configurations (AA, AB, AC, AA′, AB′, and AC′) was discussed.[61]
接下来，讨论了所有可能构型（AA, AB, AC, AA′, AB′, and AC′）的相对稳定性。[61]

All six configurations lie at the extrema.
所有六种构型都位于极值点。

It was concluded that all the materials have either the centrosymmetric AA′ or AC′ configuration as the global minima, except for GeC, where AC configuration is globally stable (refer to Fig. 7(f)).
得出结论，除了GeC中AC构型是全局稳定的之外，所有材料都具有中心对称的AA′或AC′构型作为全局最小值（参见图7(f)）。

Thus, it was predicted that for GeC, sliding ferroelectricity is experimentally more feasible.
因此，预测对于GeC，滑动铁电性在实验上更可行。

Further, the high carrier mobilities of BX bilayers are suggested, which is beneficial for faster device operation.[62]
此外，还指出了BX双层的高载流子迁移率，这有利于更快的器件操作。[62]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Carrier mobility**</span>: 载流子迁移率，指载流子（电子和空穴）在单位电场作用下的平均漂移速度。

# 2.4 Pristine and Janus transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) homobilayers
# 2.4 原始和Janus过渡金属二硫族化物同质双层
<span class="hljs-strong">**Janus transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs)**</span>: Janus过渡金属二硫族化物，指具有不对称上下表面（如硫族元素不同）的二维过渡金属二硫族化物。

Some layered materials with R3m symmetry in their ground state trigonal phase can exhibit sliding ferroelectricity in addition to bilayer configuration, such as 3R-MoS2 and γ-InSe type systems.[4]
一些在其基态三角相中具有R3m对称性的层状材料，除了双层构型外，还可以表现出滑动铁电性，例如3R-MoS2和γ-InSe型体系。[4]

In this, we discuss the sliding ferroelectricity in the TMD bilayers.
在此，我们讨论过渡金属二硫族化物双层中的滑动铁电性。

Zhang et al. discussed the sliding ferroelectricity in pristine and Janus TMDs, highlighting the enhancement in polarization in Janus TMDs compared with pristine TMDs.[63]
Zhang等人讨论了原始和Janus TMDs中的滑动铁电性，强调了与原始TMDs相比，Janus TMDs中极化的增强。[63]

The XMY-YMX (M = Mo, W; X, Y = S, Se, Te) bilayer configuration was proposed to cancel out the polarization due to the Janus asymmetry of individual layers (see Fig. 8(a) &amp; (b)).
提出XMY-YMX (M = Mo, W; X, Y = S, Se, Te) 双层构型可以抵消由于单层Janus不对称性导致的极化（见图8(a)和(b)）。

X and Y represent the exterior and interior chalcogen atoms, respectively.
X和Y分别代表外部和内部的硫族原子。

After considering six different configurations, it was concluded that the AB and AC configurations are degenerate with opposite polarization, switchable via interlayer sliding (refer to Fig. 8(c)).
在考虑了六种不同构型后，得出结论，AB和AC构型是简并的，具有相反的极化，可通过层间滑动进行翻转（参见图8(c)）。

Table 1 lists the polarization and energy barrier values for the pristine and Janus XMY-YMX bilayers.
表1列出了原始和Janus XMY-YMX双层的极化和能量势垒值。

A 65% enhancement in the polarization of the TeMoS bilayer when compared to MoS2 was reported.
据报道，与MoS2相比，TeMoS双层的极化增强了65%。

The effect of the interior chalcogen (Y) on the polarization of XMY bilayers has been emphasized (refer to Fig. 8(d) &amp; (e)).
强调了内部硫族原子(Y)对XMY双层极化的影响（参见图8(d)和(e)）。

With the higher electronegative interior chalcogen and the greatest electronegativity difference of chalcogens, TeMoS and TeWS exhibit the highest polarization among the studied bilayers.
由于内部硫族原子具有更高的电负性，并且硫族原子之间的电负性差异最大，TeMoS和TeWS在所研究的双层中表现出最高的极化。

The opposite is also true, with the smallest polarization values shown by the SMoTe and SWTe bilayers.
反之亦然，SMoTe和SWTe双层显示出最小的极化值。

Thus, the significant roles of the intrinsic electric field and the interlayer distance are highlighted.
因此，内建电场和层间距的重要作用被凸显出来。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Intrinsic electric field**</span>: 内建电场，指材料内部由于电荷分布不均匀而自发产生的电场。

Of course, the intrinsic field and interlayer distance are correlated via the interlayer interaction.
当然，内建电场和层间距通过层间相互作用相关联。

An effective distance, defined as the difference between the optimal interlayer distance and the average bond length of the metal-chalcogen bond, was introduced to understand the relationship between interlayer distance and polarization.[61]
引入了一个有效距离，定义为最佳层间距与金属-硫族元素键的平均键长之差，以理解层间距与极化之间的关系。[61]

As seen in Fig. 8(f), a negative linear relation exists between the polarization and the effective distance.
如图8(f)所示，极化与有效距离之间存在负线性关系。

Fig. 8: (a) Top and side views of the two polarized states, AB and AC, of the SeMoS bilayer.
图8: (a) SeMoS双层两个极化态AB和AC的俯视图和侧视图。

(b) Planar-averaged electric potential for the SeMoS bilayer along the z direction.
(b) SeMoS双层沿z方向的面平均电势。

(c) Energy barrier and polarization along the switching pathway AB→AC.
(c) 沿翻转路径AB→AC的能量势垒和极化。

Polarization as a function of the optimal interlayer distance for the (d) XMoY bilayer and (e) XWY bilayer.
(d) XMoY双层和(e) XWY双层的极化作为最佳层间距的函数。

(f) Relationship between polarization and effective distance.
(f) 极化与有效距离之间的关系。

(g) Variation in polarization with changes in the interlayer distance.
(g) 极化随层间距变化的变化。

(h) Energy barrier plotted against the optimal interlayer distance for the XMoY and XWY bilayers.
(h) XMoY和XWY双层能量势垒与最佳层间距的关系图。

(i) Modulation in polarization versus the energy barrier with the intrinsic field (red dots) and varying interlayer distance (blue dots), compared to MoS2 (purple dot).
(i) 极化与能量势垒的调制关系，通过内建电场（红点）和改变层间距（蓝点），与MoS2（紫点）进行对比。

(j) Variation in the energy barrier with an externally applied electric field.
(j) 能量势垒随外加电场的变化。

(k) Linear relationship between polarization and energy difference between AB and AC states per unit electric field.
(k) 极化与单位电场下AB和AC态之间能量差的线性关系。

Crystal structures of (l) 2H MoS2 bilayer, (m) 2H-like MoS2/WS2 heterobilayer, and (n) 3R-like MoS2/WS2 heterobilayer.
(l) 2H MoS2双层，(m) 类2H MoS2/WS2异质双层，以及(n) 类3R MoS2/WS2异质双层的晶体结构。

(o) Differential charge density plots for the up and down polarization states of 3R-like MoS2/WS2 heterobilayer and (p) corresponding 1d profile along the z direction.
(o) 类3R MoS2/WS2异质双层向上和向下极化态的差分电荷密度图，以及(p) 沿z方向的对应一维分布图。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [14,63]. Copyright © 2022 The American Association for the Advancement of Science. Copyright © 2022 American Chemical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[14,63]。版权所有 © 2022 美国科学促进会。版权所有 © 2022 美国化学会。

Further, the change in the interlayer distance affects the interlayer interaction and, thus, produces a change in the polarization (see Fig. 8(g)).
此外，层间距的变化会影响层间相互作用，从而引起极化的变化（见图8(g)）。

Next, the energy barrier for the ferroelectric switching is plotted against the interlayer distance (refer to Fig. 8(h)).
接下来，绘制了铁电翻转的能垒与层间距的关系图（参见图8(h)）。

The effect of the interior chalcogen atom is clearly visible by dividing the relationship into three groups: S, Se, and Te.
通过将关系分为三组：S、Se和Te，可以清楚地看到内部硫族原子的影响。

Further, the influence of intrinsic electric field and interlayer distance on the polarization vs energy barrier is compared (Fig. 8(i)).
此外，比较了内建电场和层间距对极化与能量势垒关系的影响（图8(i)）。

Distinctly, the intrinsic field effect is more dominant for exhibiting greater polarization with a smaller energy barrier as it manipulates electronic polarization rather than ionic polarization.
显然，内建电场效应在展现更大极化且具有更小能量势垒方面更占主导地位，因为它操控的是电子极化而非离子极化。

Further, to comprehend the experimental realizability of ferroelectric switching, an electric field is applied along the z direction (refer to Fig. 8(j)).
此外，为了理解铁电翻转的实验可行性，沿z方向施加了一个电场（参见图8(j)）。

The electric field uplifts the degeneracy of AB and AC states, favoring the state with polarization in the same direction as the electric field.
电场解除了AB和AC态的简并，倾向于使极化方向与电场方向相同的态。

The relationship between the ratio of electric field-induced energy difference to the electric field and the polarization is displayed in Fig. 8(k).
电场引起的能量差与电场之比与极化之间的关系显示在图8(k)中。

The greater the polarization, the greater the induced energy difference and, thus, the more susceptible the system is to the ferroelectricity switching with the electric field; this is a significant result for the understanding and the practical applications.
极化越大，诱导的能量差就越大，因此体系对由电场引起的铁电翻转就越敏感；这对于理解和实际应用来说是一个重要的结果。

An experimental study supported by density functional theory (DFT) calculations investigated sliding ferroelectricity in the MoS2/WS2 heterobilayer.[14]
一项由密度泛函理论计算支持的实验研究调查了MoS2/WS2异质双层中的滑动铁电性。[14]

Here, we discuss the theoretical results.
在这里，我们讨论理论结果。

It was demonstrated that both the 2H and 3R phases of MoS2/WS2 heterostructure possess out-of-plane polarization (see Fig. 8(l-p)).
结果表明，MoS2/WS2异质结构的2H和3R相都具有面外极化（见图8(l-p)）。

The structure belongs to the 3m (or C3v) point group, characterized by a unique rotational axis without an inversion center or a mirror plane perpendicular to it.
该结构属于3m（或C3v）点群，其特征是有一个独特的旋转轴，没有反演中心或垂直于该轴的镜面。

This symmetry is suggested to enable ferroelectricity in the system, at least from a geometric perspective.
这种对称性被认为至少从几何角度看，能使体系具有铁电性。

The polarization and energy barrier values are provided in Table 1.
极化和能量势垒值在表1中提供。

The up and down polarization states differ by a lateral distance of 1.83 Å.
向上和向下极化状态相差1.83 Å的横向距离。

The AA-up state is more stable than the AA-down state by 1.9 meV/f.u. energy.
AA-up状态比AA-down状态稳定1.9 meV/f.u.的能量。

It was reported that an electric field of 2.4 V/nm could switch the relative stability of the two states.
据报道，2.4 V/nm的电场可以翻转两种状态的相对稳定性。

Also, similar to h-BN (section 2.1), the correlation between the GPRI profile and sliding ferroelectricity for MoS2 was investigated, with Gaussian widths, σMSo = σSMo = 0.15b, where b = a/√3 and a = 3.156 Å is the lattice constant giving the best agreement of GPRI profile and DFT potential drop data.[39]
同样，与h-BN类似（第2.1节），研究了MoS2的GPRI分布与滑动铁电性之间的相关性，高斯宽度为σ_</span>Mo S = σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_S Mo = 0.15b，其中b = a/√3，a = 3.156 Å是晶格常数，使得GPRI分布与DFT势降数据最吻合。[39]

Since the structure is no longer planar, it was found that including projected Gaussian overlap between the four internal residing atoms in the PRI expression was enough.
由于结构不再是平面的，发现在PRI表达式中包含四个内部驻留原子之间的投影高斯重叠就足够了。

Thus, only the three nearest neighbors were considered for each atom, similar to the h-BN case.
因此，与h-BN情况类似，每个原子只考虑三个最近邻。

Further, the sliding ferroelectricity has been explored in the moiré-twisted MoSSe bilayer.[64]
此外，在莫尔转角的MoSSe双层中探索了滑动铁电性。[64]

Further, Cao et al. explored the PRI for the case of twisted WS2/MoS2 and WS2/MoSe2 bilayers.[39]
此外，Cao等人探索了转角WS2/MoS2和WS2/MoSe2双层的PRI。[39]

# 2.4.1 Electric control of layer-polarized spin Hall effect (LP-SHE)
# 2.4.1 层极化自旋霍尔效应的电控

Feng et al. demonstrated the correlation between the sliding ferroelectricity and the LP-SHE.[65]
Feng等人证明了滑动铁电性与层极化自旋霍尔效应之间的相关性。[65]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Layer-polarized spin Hall effect (LP-SHE)**</span>: 层极化自旋霍尔效应，一种自旋霍尔效应，其产生的自旋极化电流局限于特定层，并可通过外场进行层间切换。

It was reported that by flipping the polarization, the layer exhibiting the spin Hall effect would also exchange (refer to Fig. 9(a)).
据报道，通过翻转极化，表现出自旋霍尔效应的层也会发生交换（参见图9(a)）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Spin Hall effect (SHE)**</span>: 自旋霍尔效应，指在具有自旋-轨道耦合的材料中，注入的纵向电荷流会产生横向的自旋流。

For systems with broken inversion symmetry, there is significant spin-splitting at the K and K′ valleys with the inclusion of SOC.
对于破坏反演对称性的体系，在计入自旋轨道耦合后，K和K′谷处会出现显著的自旋劈裂。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Valley**</span>: 谷，指在半导体能带结构中，导带极小值或价带极大值在动量空间中的特定位置。

Due to the T-symmetry, the spin-splitting is valley contrasting at the K and K′ valleys.
由于时间反演对称性，自旋劈裂在K和K′谷是谷对比的。

For the polar bilayer, there is electric polarization due to sliding ferroelectricity in the z direction, which leads to an energy shift of ±U/2 for the lower (upper) layer.[65]
对于极性双层，由于z方向的滑动铁电性存在电极化，这导致下层（上层）有±U/2的能量偏移。[65]

As shown for the AB (BA) stacked MoTe2 bilayer, the lower (upper) layer contributes to the uppermost valence band maximum (VBM) (see Fig. 9(b) &amp; (c)).
如AB（BA）堆垛的MoTe2双层所示，下层（上层）对最顶部的价带顶有贡献（见图9(b)和(c)）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Valence band maximum (VBM)**</span>: 价带顶，价带中电子可以占据的最高能级。

Due to opposite Berry curvature (Ωk) at the K and K′ valleys for the AB stacked MoTe2 bilayer (Fig. 9(d)), the spin-up and down holes transverse in the opposite direction in the lower layer, with velocity v~E × Ωk, when an in-plane electric field is applied and the Fermi level is between the two spin channels at K and K′ valleys.
由于AB堆垛MoTe2双层在K和K′谷的贝里曲率相反（图9(d)），当施加面内电场且费米能级位于K和K′谷的两个自旋通道之间时，自旋向上和向下的空穴在下层中横向相反方向，速度为 v~E × Ω_</span>k。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Berry curvature**</span>: 贝里曲率，一个描述在动量空间中电子波函数几何相位性质的物理量，是产生反常速度的根源。

For the BA-stacked MoTe2 bilayer, the holes of opposite spins accumulate at the edges of the upper layer.
对于BA堆垛的MoTe2双层，相反自旋的空穴在上层边缘积累。

This is the layer-polarized spin Hall effect switchable via interlayer sliding.
这就是可通过层间滑动切换的层极化自旋霍尔效应。

It was emphasized that for systems with a large valley splitting in the conduction band, the LP-SHE is also probable at the conduction band minimum (CBM).
强调对于导带中具有大谷劈裂的体系，层极化自旋霍尔效应在导带底也是可能的。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Conduction band minimum (CBM)**</span>: 导带底，导带中电子可以占据的最低能级。

Next, the spin Hall conductivity (SHC) was calculated using the Kubo formula.[66]
接下来，使用Kubo公式计算了自旋霍尔电导率。[66]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Spin Hall conductivity (SHC)**</span>: 自旋霍尔电导率，描述材料将电荷流转化为自旋流效率的物理量。

The spin Berry curvature is the same at the K and K′ valleys (refer to Fig. 9(f)).
自旋贝里曲率在K和K′谷是相同的（参见图9(f)）。

The SHC (σxzy) ranges from -11.0 to -0.26 e/ħ S cm-1 as Fermi energy is shifted in the layers splitting gap (refer to Fig. 9(h)).
随着费米能级在层劈裂间隙中移动，自旋霍尔电导率 (σ^z<span class="hljs-emphasis">_xy) 的范围从 -11.0 到 -0.26 (e/ħ) S cm^-1（参见图9(h)）。

It was proposed that the above mechanism applies well to other systems with valley physics, too, such as MoS2, MoSe2, WS2, MoSi2N4, and WSi2N4 (section 2.6).
提出上述机制也很好地适用于其他具有谷物理的体系，如MoS2, MoSe2, WS2, MoSi2N4和WSi2N4（第2.6节）。

The effect of strain on the layer-polarized band structure of the MoS2 bilayer is displayed in Fig. 9(i).
应变对MoS2双层能带结构层极化影响显示在图9(i)中。

With the -3% strain, the VBM shifts from the Γ to the K valleys, thus enabling the realization of LP-SHE under the in-plane electric field (see Fig. 9(j)).
在-3%的应变下，价带顶从Γ谷转移到K谷，从而使得在面内电场下实现层极化自旋霍尔效应成为可能（见图9(j)）。

Fig. 9: A schematic representation of layer-polarized spin Hall effect (LP-SHE) for hole-doped (left) and electron-doped (right) polar stacked bilayers exhibiting sliding ferroelectricity.
图9: 显示滑动铁电性的极性堆垛双层中，空穴掺杂（左）和电子掺杂（右）的层极化自旋霍尔效应示意图。

(b) and (c) Layer-polarized band structures for AB and BA stacked MoTe2 bilayer, respectively.
(b) 和 (c) 分别为AB和BA堆垛MoTe2双层的层极化能带结构。

(d) Berry curvature near the valence band maximum (VBM) along high-symmetry lines of the MoTe2 bilayer.
(d) MoTe2双层沿高对称线的价带顶附近的贝里曲率。

(f) Spin Berry curvature-projected band structure and k-resolved spin Berry curvature along high-symmetry lines at E1.
(f) 在能量E1处，沿高对称线的自旋贝里曲率投影能带结构和k分辨的自旋贝里曲率。

(g) Spin Hall conductivity (SHC) as a function of Fermi energy, with (h) an enlarged view near the VBM.
(g) 自旋霍尔电导率作为费米能量的函数，以及(h) 价带顶附近的放大视图。

(i) Band structure variation under applied strain in the MoS2 bilayer.
(i) MoS2双层在外加应变下的能带结构变化。

(j) SHC as a function of Fermi energy under -3% strain for the MoS2 bilayer.
(j) 在-3%应变下，MoS2双层的自旋霍尔电导率作为费米能量的函数。
