经许可重印自参考文献[65]。版权所有 © 2024 美国化学会。

# 2.5 1T′-ReS2 multilayers
# 2.5 1T′-ReS2 多层

Fig. 10: (a) Top and side views of the ReSe2 monolayer.
图10: (a) ReSe2单层的俯视图和侧视图。

(b) Top and side views of the ferroelectric bi-stable states A and A′, along with the nonpolar intermediate B state for the ReS2 bilayer.
(b) ReS2双层的铁电双稳态A和A′，以及非极性中间态B的俯视图和侧视图。

(c) Energy contour plot for the ReSe2 bilayer as a function of the sliding distances (la, lb).
(c) ReSe2双层能量作为滑动距离(la, lb)函数的等高线图。

(d) Energy barrier for ferroelectric switching between the states A and A′.
(d) 状态A和A′之间铁电翻转的能量势垒。

(e) Charge density difference plot, with yellow (blue) bubbles indicating the accumulation (depletion) of electrons.
(e) 电荷密度差图，黄色（蓝色）气泡表示电子的积累（耗尽）。

Illustration of ferroelectric switching in ReS2 (f) trilayer and (g) tetralayer.
ReS2 (f)三层和(g)四层中铁电翻转的示意图。

(h) Variation of polarization and energy barrier as a function of the layer number.
(h) 极化和能量势垒随层数的变化。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [15]. Copyright © 2022 American Physical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[15]。版权所有 © 2022 美国物理学会。

Wan et al. theoretically and experimentally explored the sliding ferroelectricity in the 1T'-ReS2 multilayers.[15]
Wan等人从理论和实验上探索了1T'-ReS2多层中的滑动铁电性。[15]

Here, only the theoretical results are discussed.
在这里，只讨论理论结果。

1T'-ReS2 has a structure similar to 1Td -WS2 (discussed in section 2.2) and β-ZrI2 (discussed in section 3.2) (see Fig. 10(a)).
1T'-ReS2具有与1Td -WS2（第2.2节讨论）和β-ZrI2（第3.2节讨论）相似的结构（见图10(a)）。

The monolayer possesses inversion symmetry.
单层具有反演对称性。

The stacking of two layers and probing the energy landscape by sliding reveals two degenerate states with opposite polarization (refer to Fig. 10(b) &amp; (c)).
堆叠两层并通过滑动来探测能量景观，揭示了两个具有相反极化的简并态（参见图10(b)和(c)）。

The berry phase calculation confirmed the ferroelectricity with a polarization value of 0.07 pC.m-1 and an energy barrier of 17.1 meV (see Fig. 10(d)).
贝里相位计算证实了铁电性，极化值为0.07 pC·m^-1，能量势垒为17.1 meV（见图10(d)）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Berry phase**</span>: 贝里相位，指系统在绝热演化过程中获得的几何相位，在现代极化理论中用于计算电极化。

The charge density difference analysis unveiled a charge transfer of 0.0003e (refer to Fig. 10(e)) from the top to the bottom layer, leading to out-of-plane polarization in the system.
电荷密度差分析揭示，从顶层到底层有0.0003e的电荷转移（参见图10(e)），导致体系中产生面外极化。

Next, vertical ferroelectricity was investigated using an increased number of layers.
接下来，通过增加层数来研究垂直铁电性。

It was proposed that for the trilayer system, sliding the middle layer by (1 Å, 3.6 Å) would result in the configuration with opposite polarization (refer to Fig. 10(f)).
提出对于三层体系，将中间层滑动(1 Å, 3.6 Å)会产生具有相反极化的构型（参见图10(f)）。

Similarly, for the tetralayer system, the sliding of the second and fourth layers by (1 Å, 3.6 Å) leads to the state with reversed polarization (see Fig. 10(g)).
类似地，对于四层体系，第二层和第四层滑动(1 Å, 3.6 Å)会导致极化翻转的状态（见图10(g)）。

The variation in the polarization and energy barrier versus the layer number is shown in Fig. 10(h).
极化和能量势垒随层数的变化显示在图10(h)中。

The polarization ranges from 0.07-0.68 pC.m-1 as the layer number increases from 2 to 7.
随着层数从2增加到7，极化范围从0.07到0.68 pC·m^-1。

As the layer number increases, the corresponding energy barrier rises from 17 to 100 meV.
随着层数的增加，相应的能量势垒从17 meV上升到100 meV。

# 2.6 MoA2N4 (A = Si, Ge) bilayer
# 2.6 MoA2N4 (A = Si, Ge) 双层

Fig. 11: (a) Two equivalent crystal structures with opposite polarizations for the bilayer MoSi2N4 and MoGe2N4.
图11: (a) 双层MoSi2N4和MoGe2N4的两个具有相反极化的等效晶体结构。

(b) Ferroelectric switching pathway for the bilayer MoSi2N4 and MoGe2N4 calculated using the NEB method.
(b) 使用NEB方法计算的双层MoSi2N4和MoGe2N4的铁电翻转路径。

(c) Distributions of the VBM and CBM bands, and (d) the partial density of states (PDOS) for the bilayer MoSi2N4.
(c) 双层MoSi2N4的价带顶和导带底能带分布，以及(d) 分态密度。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Partial density of states (PDOS)**</span>: 分态密度，指态密度在不同原子或不同轨道上的投影，用于分析各组分对能带的贡献。

(e) Band alignment representation across different stacking regions in the Moiré superlattice of a twisted MoSi2N4 bilayer.
(e) 转角MoSi2N4双层莫尔超晶格中不同堆垛区域的能带对齐示意图。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [67]. Copyright © 2021 Royal Society of Chemistry.
经许可重印自参考文献[67]。版权所有 © 2021 皇家化学学会。

The MoA2N4 (A = Si, Ge) monolayer crystallizes in a hexagonal lattice with space group symmetry P̅6m2.[54]
MoA2N4 (A = Si, Ge) 单层结晶为六方晶格，空间群对称性为P̅6m2。[54]

The structure can be considered as the 2H-MoN2 trilayer sandwiched between the two Si-N bilayers.
该结构可被视为夹在两个Si-N双层之间的2H-MoN2三层。

The MoA2N4 monolayer possesses a horizontal mirror symmetry Mz with Mo at the center; thus, vertical polarization is not allowed.
MoA2N4单层具有水平镜面对称性Mz，Mo位于中心；因此，不允许垂直极化存在。

On the other hand, the MoA2N4 bilayer exists in an AB or AC-stacked ground state configuration (see Fig. 11(a) &amp; (b)) and, hence, exhibits out-of-plane polarization of 3.36 pC.m-1 and 3.05 pC.m-1 for the MoSi2N4 and MoGe2N4 bilayer, respectively.[53]
另一方面，MoA2N4双层以AB或AC堆垛的基态构型存在（见图11(a)和(b)），因此，MoSi2N4和MoGe2N4双层分别表现出3.36 pC·m^-1和3.05 pC·m^-1的面外极化。[53]

The isostructural CrSi2N4 and WSi2N4 bilayer were also reported to possess vertical polarization of 2.49 pC.m-1 and 3.44 pC.m-1, respectively.[53]
据报道，同构的CrSi2N4和WSi2N4双层也分别具有2.49 pC·m^-1和3.44 pC·m^-1的垂直极化。[53]

The higher magnitude of vertical polarization in the MoA2N4 bilayer compared to the WTe2 type of systems is attributed to the greater interlayer charge transfer, with the areal charge density reaching the value of 1.31 μC.cm-2 for the MoSi2N4 bilayer.
与WTe2型体系相比，MoA2N4双层中垂直极化幅度更高，这归因于更大的层间电荷转移，MoSi2N4双层的面电荷密度达到1.31 μC·cm^-2。

Further, the high electron mobility of 7990 cm2.V-1.s-1 was reported using Bardeen and Shockey’s deformation potential approach.
此外，使用Bardeen和Shockey的形变势方法，报道了7990 cm^2·V^-1·s^-1的高电子迁移率。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Deformation potential approach**</span>: 形变势方法，一种计算载流子迁移率的理论方法，考虑了晶格振动（声子）对载流子的散射作用。

Thus, the MoA2N4 bilayers could be better candidates for practical applications than the h-BN and WTe2 systems.
因此，MoA2N4双层可能比h-BN和WTe2体系更适合实际应用。

Now, the analysis of the atomic projected partial density of states at the VBM and CBM suggests that one layer contributes to the VBM while the other layer contributes to the CBM (see Fig. 11(c) &amp; (d)).
现在，对价带顶和导带底处原子投影分态密度的分析表明，一层对价带顶有贡献，而另一层对导带底有贡献（见图11(c)和(d)）。

Also, the layer contribution to the VBM and CBM flips with the switching of the FE polarization (refer to Fig. 11(e)).
此外，层对价带顶和导带底的贡献随着铁电极化的翻转而翻转（参见图11(e)）。

For the moiré superlattice formed by small-angle twisting, different regions display band alignment similar to a quantum dot array with type-II heterojunctions, both in-plane as well as out-of-plane, thus, can serve as a platform to facilitate exciton trapping.
对于由小角度扭转形成的莫尔超晶格，不同区域显示出类似于量子点阵列的能带对齐，具有面内和面外的II型异质结，因此，可以作为促进激子捕获的平台。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Type-II heterojunctions**</span>: II型异质结，一种能带排列方式，其中一种材料的导带底和价带顶都低于另一种材料，有利于电子和空穴的空间分离。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Exciton**</span>: 激子，由库仑相互作用束缚在一起的电子-空穴对。

It was proposed that, in the present case, the moiré potential originated from vertical polarization rather than interlayer hybridization, as per conventional understanding.[67,68]
提出在目前的情况下，与传统理解不同，莫尔势源于垂直极化而非层间杂化。[67,68]

Further, the investigation of the variation in FE polarization with the layer number suggested an increase in the polarization with the thickness of the material.[67]
此外，对铁电极化随层数变化的研究表明，极化随材料厚度的增加而增加。[67]

Additionally, it was predicted that the MoN2/MoSi2N4 heterobilayer could also be utilized for the nanogenerator application, with the charge on MoN2 varying with the interlayer sliding.[67]
此外，预测MoN2/MoSi2N4异质双层也可用于纳米发电机应用，其中MoN2上的电荷随层间滑动而变化。[67]

# 2.7 HgX2 (X= Br, I)
# 2.7 HgX2 (X= Br, I)

Fig. 12: (a) Top and side views of the crystal structures of polar (±P) and non-polar (PE) bulk HgX2.
图12: (a) 极性(±P)和非极性(PE)块体HgX2晶体结构的俯视图和侧视图。

(b) Energy double-well profile and polarization behaviour along the switching pathway for bulk HgX2.
(b) 块体HgX2沿翻转路径的能量双阱分布和极化行为。

(c) Energy double-well profile for the HgX2 bilayer system.
(c) HgX2双层体系的能量双阱分布。

(d) Planar-averaged screening charge profiles for the HgI2 multilayers comprising three, four, five, and six layers.
(d) 包含三、四、五和六层的HgI2多层的面平均屏蔽电荷分布。

(e) Total interlayer polarization and (f) average interlayer polarization as a function of the number of HgI2 layers.
(e) 总层间极化和(f) 平均层间极化作为HgI2层数的函数。

(g) Spin texture for the VBM and CBM in the kx − ky reciprocal plane for the +P state of the FE-HgI2 bilayer.
(g) FE-HgI2双层+P态在kx-ky倒易平面中价带顶和导带底的自旋织构。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [69]. Copyright © 2024 American Chemical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[69]。版权所有 © 2024 美国化学会。

Bulk HgX2 (X= Br, I) belonging to the space group Cmc21 exists with anti-parallel stacking (refer to Fig. 12(a)).
块体HgX2 (X= Br, I) 属于空间群Cmc21，以反平行堆垛形式存在（参见图12(a)）。

Chen et al. discussed the potential of HgX2 in sliding ferroelectric switching at the bulk and bilayer levels (refer to Fig. 12(b) &amp; (c)).[69]
Chen等人讨论了HgX2在块体和双层水平的滑动铁电翻转潜力（参见图12(b)和(c)）。[69]

Additionally, the HgI2 bilayer exhibits the Rashba effect, opening a pathway for low-cost spintronic applications.
此外，HgI2双层表现出拉什巴效应，为低成本的自旋电子学应用开辟了道路。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Rashba effect**</span>: 拉什巴效应，指在缺乏反演对称性的体系中，自旋轨道耦合导致能带发生自旋劈裂的现象。

The polarization of the bilayer was computed using the method based on the integration of planar averaged screened charge density difference (Δρ) of FE and PE phases.
双层的极化计算采用基于积分铁电相和顺电相的面平均屏蔽电荷密度差(Δρ)的方法。

The polarization P is evaluated by the expression: P = m/d (eq. 15), where m is the dipole moment and d is the thickness of the 2D HgX2 layers.
极化P通过表达式 P = m/d (eq. 15) 计算，其中m是偶极矩，d是二维HgX2层的厚度。

The dipole moment (m) relates to the screened charge density difference (Δρ) (shown in Fig. 12(d)) as:
偶极矩(m)与屏蔽电荷密度差(Δρ)（如图12(d)所示）的关系为：

m = ∫ Δρ(z). z dz
+∞
−∞ (eq. 16)
m = ∫_</span>{-∞}^{+∞} Δρ(z)·z dz (eq. 16)

The phonon spectrum and molecular dynamics performed at 300 K revealed the dynamical stability and the existence of room-temperature ferroelectricity, respectively.
在300 K下计算的声子谱和分子动力学模拟分别揭示了其动力学稳定性和室温铁电性的存在。

Also, a weak depolarization field of -0.013 V/Å (predicted as the slope of macroscopic electrostatic potential) is not enough to overcome the energy barrier of 24 meV, thus confirming the robust nature of ferroelectric polarization in the HgI2 bilayer.
此外，-0.013 V/Å的弱退极化场（预测为宏观静电势的斜率）不足以克服24 meV的能量势垒，从而证实了HgI2双层中铁电极化的稳健特性。

Further, the polarization with the number of layers was examined.
此外，还检查了极化与层数的关系。

It was predicted that the depolarization field and the polarization arise from the surface polar charges at the top and bottom surfaces of the multilayer system.
据预测，退极化场和极化源于多层体系顶部和底部表面的表面极性电荷。

The integration over the monolayer regions cancels out, indicating the absence of contribution from cation displacement to sliding ferroelectricity in the HgI2 multilayer system.
在单层区域上的积分抵消了，这表明在HgI2多层体系中，阳离子位移对滑动铁电性没有贡献。

Since the average interlayer polarization decreases with the thickness, the total polarization increases but not monotonously with the layer number (refer to Fig. 12(e) &amp; (f)).
由于平均层间极化随厚度减小，总极化随层数增加但非单调增加（参见图12(e)和(f)）。

It was suggested that HgI2 nanoflakes can exhibit a saturated polarization of 0.16 μC/cm2.
这表明HgI2纳米片可以表现出0.16 μC/cm^2的饱和极化。

Next, the SOC band structure revealed a spin-splitting of energy bands.
接下来，自旋轨道耦合能带结构揭示了能带的自旋劈裂。

The spin textures near the VBM and CBM unveiled the presence of the Rashba effect, with an anti-clockwise spin orientation in the CBM-inner and VBM-outer and a clockwise spin orientation in the CBM-outer and VBM-inner (see Fig. 12(g)).
价带顶和导带底附近的自旋织构揭示了拉什巴效应的存在，其中导带底内层和价带顶外层为逆时针自旋取向，而导带底外层和价带顶内层为顺时针自旋取向（见图12(g)）。

If spin is injected from the magnetic source with spin orienting along the z direction, the Rashba SOC field tries to align it along the y-axis (in-plane).
如果从磁源注入自旋，且自旋取向沿z方向，拉什巴自旋轨道耦合场会试图将其排列到y轴（面内）方向。

Depending on the parallel or antiparallel spin direction with respect to the detector, the device is either in an ON or OFF state, respectively.[59]
根据相对于检测器的自旋方向是平行还是反平行，器件分别处于ON或OFF状态。[59]

Further, with interlayer sliding, the sign of polarization reverses, the intrinsic electric field reverses, and the spin-orientation reverses, thus the interlayer sliding control of the proposed device.
此外，随着层间滑动，极化的符号反转，内建电场反转，自旋取向也反转，从而实现了对所提出器件的层间滑动控制。

<span class="hljs-section"># 2.8 GeS2/CuInP2S6</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 2.8 GeS2/CuInP2S6</span>

Sun et al. explored the coupling between the displacement and sliding ferroelectricity in the GeS2/CuInP2S6 and GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2 heterostructures and their moiré superlattices.[70]
Sun等人探索了GeS2/CuInP2S6和GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2异质结构及其莫尔超晶格中位移铁电性与滑动铁电性之间的耦合。[70]

The GeS2 monolayer belonging to the space group P31m is non-polar, while the CuInP2S6 (CIPS) monolayer is ferroelectric with space group symmetry P3.
属于空间群P31m的GeS2单层是非极性的，而CuInP2S6 (CIPS)单层是铁电的，空间群对称性为P3。

For the case of the GeS2/CuInP2S6 bilayer, due to the Cu ion displacement and sliding of the GeS2 layer, four polarization states with high polarization (HP±) and low polarization (LP±) exist as shown in Fig. 13(a).
对于GeS2/CuInP2S6双层，由于Cu离子位移和GeS2层的滑动，存在四个极化状态，即高极化(HP±)和低极化(LP±)，如图13(a)所示。

The polarization states HP (±) and LP (±) are related via interlayer sliding.
极化态HP(±)和LP(±)通过层间滑动相关联。

In contrast, the HP(LP)+ and HP(LP)− polarization states are linked via the Cu ion displacement.
相反，HP(LP)+和HP(LP)−极化态通过Cu离子位移相关联。

Fig. 13(b) shows the polarizations and energy barriers for the four states encountered along the transition pathway.
图13(b)显示了沿转变路径遇到的四种状态的极化和能量势垒。

The room temperature stability of the ferroelectric states was confirmed through the Ab initio molecular dynamics.
通过从头算分子动力学模拟确认了铁电态的室温稳定性。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD)**</span>: 从头算分子动力学，一种基于第一性原理计算电子结构，并实时求解原子运动方程的方法。

The displacement of Cu ions was observed to not only reverse the polarization direction in CIPS but also alter the system's built-in electric field, resulting in interfacial charge redistribution.
观察到Cu离子的位移不仅反转了CIPS中的极化方向，还改变了体系的内建电场，导致界面电荷重新分布。

Consequently, the sliding ferroelectricity, which depends on the interlayer charge, is strongly coupled with displacement ferroelectricity in the GeS2/CuInP2S6 system.
因此，依赖于层间电荷的滑动铁电性与GeS2/CuInP2S6体系中的位移铁电性强烈耦合。

For the GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2 heterostructure, due to two slidable GeS2 layers, six polarization states HP±, LP1±, and LP2± are probable, with symmetric polarization of ±11.77 pC.m-1 for the HP± states.[70]
对于GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2异质结构，由于有两个可滑动的GeS2层，可能存在六个极化状态HP±、LP1±和LP2±，其中HP±态的极化对称值为±11.77 pC·m^-1。[70]

The higher polarization was reported to result from the greater displacement of Cu ions influenced by the bottom GeS2 layer (refer to Fig. 13(c) &amp; (d)).
据报道，更高的极化是由于受到底部GeS2层影响的Cu离子产生了更大的位移（参见图13(c)和(d)）。

Next, the moiré pattern of the trilayer heterostructure was formed with periodicity A ≈ a/√δ2 + θ2, where a is the lattice constant of the unit cell, δ is the lattice mismatch and θ is the twist angle (see Fig. 13(e)).
接下来，三层异质结构的莫尔图案形成，其周期为 A ≈ a/√(δ^2 + θ^2)，其中a是晶胞的晶格常数，δ是晶格失配，θ是转角（见图13(e)）。

Three equidistant and high symmetry regions, R0, R1, and R2, were identified, and regions R1 and R2 can be viewed as formed from the sliding of the two GeS2 layers in the R0 region (refer to Fig. 13(f) &amp; (g)).
确定了三个等距的高对称区域R0、R1和R2，区域R1和R2可以看作是由R0区域中两个GeS2层滑动形成的（参见图13(f)和(g)）。

Due to the sliding ferroelectricity, the moiré superlattice experiences effective electrostatic moiré potential, leading to periodic polarization (refer to Fig. 13(h)).
由于滑动铁电性，莫尔超晶格会经历有效的静电莫尔势，从而导致周期性极化（参见图13(h)）。

The calculated transition energy barrier of 286 meV remains almost the same in all regions.
计算得到的286 meV转变能量势垒在所有区域几乎保持不变。

Notably, coupling Cu ion displacement (via electric field) with the sliding ferroelectricity enables the otherwise improbable ferroelectric switching in moiré superlattice.
值得注意的是，将Cu离子位移（通过电场）与滑动铁电性耦合，使得在莫尔超晶格中原本不可能实现的铁电翻转成为可能。

Further, the storage density of 0.7 TB cm-2 for the ferroelectric array was proposed for the 5° twist angle.[70]
此外，对于5°转角，提出了铁电阵列0.7 TB cm^-2的存储密度。[70]

Fig. 13: (a) Ferroelectric switching paths in the GeS2/CuInP2S6 heterostructure, involving both layer sliding and ion displacement mechanisms.
图13: (a) GeS2/CuInP2S6异质结构中的铁电翻转路径，涉及层滑动和离子位移两种机制。

(b) Polarization and energy barrier profiles for the transition from the HP+ state to the HP- state in the heterostructure.
(b) 异质结构中从HP+态到HP-态转变的极化和能量势垒曲线。

(c) Differential charge density (Δρ) along the z direction and charge transfer (n) for the GeS2/CuInP2S6 and GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2 heterostructures.
(c) GeS2/CuInP2S6和GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2异质结构沿z方向的差分电荷密度(Δρ)和电荷转移量(n)。

(d) Energy barrier for Cu ion displacement along the black dashed line in (c) for bilayer and trilayer heterostructures.
(d) 对于双层和三层异质结构，沿(c)中黑色虚线所示的Cu离子位移的能量势垒。

(e) Schematic representation of the moiré superlattice of GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2 heterostructure, and (f) DFT-calculated moiré pattern along with the amplified three local regions R0, R1, and R2 of the stacking configurations.
(e) GeS2/CuInP2S6/GeS2异质结构莫尔超晶格的示意图，以及(f) DFT计算的莫尔图案，其中放大了堆垛构型的三个局部区域R0、R1和R2。

(g) Polarization textures in the P+ and P- states and (h) periodic polarization magnitude while traversing the R2 regions.
(g) P+和P-态中的极化织构，以及(h) 穿过R2区域时的周期性极化大小。

(i) Transition energy barriers in the local R0, R1, and R2 regions as a function of the Cu ion displacement distance during the polarization transition.
(i) 局部R0、R1和R2区域在极化转变过程中，转变能量势垒作为Cu离子位移距离的函数。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [70]. Copyright © 2023 American Chemical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[70]。版权所有 © 2023 美国化学会。

<span class="hljs-section"># 2.9 C/BN multilayers</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 2.9 C/BN 多层</span>

Although sliding ferroelectricity is widespread in 2D materials, for the mono-elemental bilayers, sliding with any interlayer vector does not break the inversion symmetry.
尽管滑动铁电性在二维材料中广泛存在，但对于单质双层，任何层间矢量滑动都不会破坏反演对称性。

Yang et al. proposed across-layer sliding ferroelectricity (ALSF) arising from the next-neighbor asymmetric interlayer coupling to overcome this limitation.[71]
Yang等人提出了由次近邻不对称层间耦合引起的跨层滑动铁电性，以克服这一限制。[71]

Parallel h-BN layers with intercalated graphene bilayer heterostructure was reported to break inversion symmetry and exhibit sliding ferroelectricity.
据报道，插层石墨烯双层异质结构的平行h-BN层会破坏反演对称性并表现出滑动铁电性。

Although in both polar states, the C atom is always facing the B atom, the difference lies in the equivalence shifting from (AA, AB) to (AB, AA), as shown in Fig. 14(a).
尽管在两种极性态中，C原子总是面对B原子，但区别在于等价性从(AA, AB)转移到(AB, AA)，如图14(a)所示。

The bipolar states correlated by mirror symmetry have electric polarization of 0.48 pC.m-1 with NEB calculated 3 meV/u.c. energy barrier separating the states (refer to Fig. 14(b)).
由镜像对称性关联的双极态具有0.48 pC·m^-1的电极化，NEB计算表明分隔两态的能垒为3 meV/u.c.（参见图14(b)）。

The switching is achieved by sliding the upper two layers w.r.t. the bottom layers.
翻转是通过使上面两层相对于下面两层滑动来实现的。

The reversal in the polarization is visible in the differential charge density plots of Fig. 14(c).
极化的反转在图14(c)的差分电荷密度图中可见。

Fig. 14: (a) Geometric structure of the two ferroelectric states formed by intercalated graphene between two parallel h-BN layers.
图14: (a) 由插入在两个平行h-BN层之间的石墨烯形成的两种铁电态的几何结构。

(b) Energy barrier profile for ferroelectric switching, and (c) differential charge density plots for the two bipolar states.
(b) 铁电翻转的能垒曲线，以及(c) 两种双极态的差分电荷密度图。

(d) Independent molecular behavior enables the benzene molecular layer/graphene/h-BN heterostructure to achieve high-density storage through sliding ferroelectric switching.
(d) 独立的分子行为使得苯分子层/石墨烯/h-BN异质结构能够通过滑动铁电翻转实现高密度存储。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [71]. Copyright © 2023 Wiley-VCH GmbH.
经许可重印自参考文献[71]。版权所有 © 2023 Wiley-VCH GmbH。

With the increase in h-BN layers thickness, the system still exhibits ALSF with slightly increased polarization of 0.51 pC.m-1.
随着h-BN层厚度的增加，该体系仍然表现出跨层滑动铁电性，极化略微增加到0.51 pC·m^-1。

An earlier experimental report on the h-BN layer over a graphene bilayer suggested charge transfer due to electronic correlations as a reason for the unconventional ferroelectricity exhibited by the system.[72]
早先一篇关于石墨烯双层上h-BN层的实验报告提出，由于电子关联导致的电荷转移是该体系表现出非常规铁电性的原因。[72]

Yang et al. proposed that ALSF could be one of the causes of ferroelectricity in such systems.
Yang等人提出，跨层滑动铁电性可能是此类体系产生铁电性的原因之一。

For the moiré pattern, it was emphasized that the structure would reconstruct itself to achieve the lowest energy configuration domains with narrow domain walls.
对于莫尔图案，强调结构会自我重构，以形成具有窄畴壁的最低能量构型畴。

Next, the two and three graphene layers over h-BN systems were also discussed to achieve ALSF.[71]
接下来，还讨论了h-BN体系上的两层和三层石墨烯以实现跨层滑动铁电性。[71]

Since the data storage capacity is limited due to the simultaneous sliding of a large-area layer, replacing the upper graphene layer in the two graphene layers over h-BN with a molecular layer, such as benzene, was proposed (refer to Fig. 14(d)).
由于大面积层同时滑动导致数据存储容量有限，因此提出用分子层（如苯）替换h-BN上两层石墨烯中的上层石墨烯层（参见图14(d)）。

As a molecule can independently store a bit, an ultrahigh areal density of 104 Tbit per square inch was predicted for the benzene/graphene/h-BN heterostructure.
由于一个分子可以独立存储一个比特，预计苯/石墨烯/h-BN异质结构可实现每平方英寸10^4 Tbit的超高面密度。

<span class="hljs-section"># 2.10 Graphene multilayers (N &gt; 3)</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 2.10 石墨烯多层 (N &gt; 3)</span>

Fig. 15: (a) Switching among polar stacking states ABAC, CABA, and CBAB in four-layer graphene, and (b) the corresponding ferroelectric switching pathway, calculated by the NEB method.
图15: (a) 四层石墨烯中极性堆垛态ABAC、CABA和CBAB之间的翻转，以及(b) 通过NEB方法计算的相应铁电翻转路径。

(c) Differential charge density distributions for the polar states, along with Hirschfeld charge analysis.
(c) 极性态的差分电荷密度分布，以及Hirschfeld电荷分析。

Yellow and blue isosurfaces represent the electron accumulation and depletion caused by layer stacking.
黄色和蓝色等值面表示由层堆叠引起的电子积累和耗尽。

(d) Stacking domain chart for 2 + 2 and 1 + 3 twisted tetralayer graphene, where NP and X denote the nonpolar domains and energetically unfavorable domains.
(d) 2+2和1+3转角四层石墨烯的堆垛畴图，其中NP和X分别表示非极性畴和能量上不利的畴。

The band structures for the (e) untwisted and (f) 1.08° twisted tetralayer graphene.
(e) 未转角和(f) 1.08° 转角四层石墨烯的能带结构。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [73]. Copyright © 2023 American Physical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[73]。版权所有 © 2023 美国物理学会。

The graphene monolayer and bilayer are centrosymmetric, while the trilayer possesses either a mirror symmetry or inversion symmetry, resulting in zero vertical polarization.
石墨烯单层和双层是中心对称的，而三层具有镜面对称或反演对称，导致垂直极化为零。

For tetralayer graphene, however, some stacking sequences are polar and exhibit ALSF, similar to the C/BN multilayers as discussed in the previous section.[73]
然而，对于四层石墨烯，某些堆垛序列是极性的，并表现出跨层滑动铁电性，类似于前节讨论的C/BN多层。[73]

The configurations ABAB and ABCB are non-polar, while the stacking sequence ABAC, CABA, and CBAB are polar and correlated by mirror symmetry (refer to Fig. 15(a)).
构型ABAB和ABCB是非极性的，而堆垛序列ABAC、CABA和CBAB是极性的，并通过镜像对称相关联（参见图15(a)）。

The configurations with vertical ferroelectric polarization of 0.21 pC.m-1 and in-plane polarization of 57.49 pC.m-1, are interchangeable via sliding with energy barriers &lt;5 meV (see Fig. 15(b)).
具有0.21 pC·m^-1垂直铁电极化和57.49 pC·m^-1面内极化的构型，可以通过滑动相互转换，能量势垒小于5 meV（见图15(b)）。

Further, the charge and charge density analysis are consistent with the ferroelectric polarization switching between ABAC and CBAB states, as shown in Fig. 15(c).
此外，电荷和电荷密度分析与ABAC和CBAB状态之间的铁电极化翻转一致，如图15(c)所示。

Similarly, for the pentalayer graphene structure, ABABC and ABACB polar states with vertical polarization of 0.17 pC.m-1 and 0.32 pC.m-1, respectively, are connected via interlayer sliding to the other non-polar states.
类似地，对于五层石墨烯结构，具有垂直极化0.17 pC·m^-1和0.32 pC·m^-1的ABABC和ABACB极性态，通过层间滑动与其他非极性态相连。

Interestingly, the hexalayer graphene multilayer system has five polar states with vertical polarization lying in the range of 0.05 pC.m-1 to 1.0 pC.m-1.
有趣的是，六层石墨烯多层体系有五个极性态，垂直极化范围在0.05 pC·m^-1到1.0 pC·m^-1之间。

Thus, it was suggested that the more layer configurations with ASLF may find application in the memristor devices.
因此，表明具有跨层滑动铁电性的更多层构型可能在忆阻器器件中找到应用。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Memristor**</span>: 忆阻器，一种具有记忆功能的非线性电阻器，其阻值依赖于流经的电荷量。

Next, small-angle twisted tetralayer graphene was explored for its potential for vertical polarization.
接下来，探索了小角度转角四层石墨烯的垂直极化潜力。

For the twisting center located in the middle, i.e., 2 + 2 case, the AB + AB stacking sequence twist was predicted to lead to only non-polar and unstable configurations.
对于位于中间的扭转中心，即2+2情况，预测AB + AB堆垛序列扭转只会导致非极性和不稳定的构型。

The BA + AB twist would lead to two opposite polarization state domains, hence, net zero polarization of the system.
BA + AB扭转会导致两个相反的极化态畴，因此，体系的净极化为零。

On the other hand, 1 + 3 twisted graphene, with 1 + ABA twist, has non-polar (BABA) and up-polarized (CABA) domains, resulting in a non-zero vertical polarization in the structure.
另一方面，1+3转角石墨烯，其中1+ABA扭转，具有非极性(BABA)和向上极化(CABA)的畴，导致结构中具有非零的垂直极化。

Similarly, the 1 + ABC twist leads to non-polar (CABC) and down-polarized (BABC) type domains with non-zero vertical polarization (refer to Fig. 15(d)).
类似地，1+ABC扭转导致非极性(CABC)和向下极化(BABC)型畴，具有非零的垂直极化（参见图15(d)）。

Now, the switching of the ferroelectric polarization can be achieved with the application of an electric field.
现在，可以通过施加电场来实现铁电极化的翻转。

The electric field may transfer the polar domain (CABA) to the non-polar domain (CABC) with the bottom layer translation, while simultaneously changing the non-polar (BABA) domain to the polar (BABC) domain, thus, allowing the switching of polarization in the moiré structure and is termed as “sliding moiré ferroelectricity”.
电场可以通过底层平移将极性畴(CABA)转变为非极性畴(CABC)，同时将非极性畴(BABA)转变为极性畴(BABC)，从而允许在莫尔结构中翻转极化，这被称为“滑动莫尔铁电性”。

Further, the band structure investigation revealed different behavior of various stacking sequences near the Fermi level.
此外，能带结构研究揭示了不同堆垛序列在费米能级附近的不同行为。

The polar CABC state has flat bands near the Fermi level, the non-polar ABAB state is metallic, and the polar ABAC state has a linear dispersion band and a flat band near the Fermi level (see Fig. 15(e)).
极性CABC态在费米能级附近具有平带，非极性ABAB态是金属性的，而极性ABAC态在费米能级附近具有一个线性色散带和一个平带（见图15(e)）。

For the twisted graphene scenario, the 1 + ABA twist band structure has an extra linear dispersion band at the K edge, in addition to flat bands at the Γ edge also observed for the 1 + ABA twist case (refer to Fig. 15(f)).
对于转角石墨烯的情况，1+ABA扭转带结构在K边界处有一个额外的线性色散带，此外在Γ边界处还观察到平带，这在1+ABA扭转情况下也观察到了（参见图15(f)）。

Thus, uncovering their different transport properties, it was suggested that the transport behavior of states may prove helpful in their experimental identification.
因此，揭示了它们不同的输运性质，表明这些状态的输运行为可能有助于通过实验识别它们。

<span class="hljs-section"># 2.11 1-uc thick BTO on SnS</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 2.11 单胞厚钛酸钡在硫化锡上</span>

The issue of critical thickness still persists in three-dimensional (3D) ferroelectrics.
临界厚度问题在三维铁电体中仍然存在。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Critical thickness**</span>: 临界厚度，指铁电薄膜保持铁电性所需的最小厚度，低于此厚度铁电性会因退极化场而消失。

Zhang et al. proposed a technique of 2D/3D hybrid ferroelectric heterostructure (1unit-cell thick BaTiO3 on SnS) for the stabilization of ferroelectric polarization in the 3D ferroelectric.[74]
Zhang等人提出了一种二维/三维杂化铁电异质结构（单胞厚BaTiO3在SnS上）技术，用于稳定三维铁电体中的铁电极化。[74]

Due to necessity of paraelectric phase existence, the planar (2D) BTO layer should be symmetric.
由于需要存在顺电相，平面（二维）BaTiO3层应该是对称的。

Thus, two cases, BaO and TiO2 terminations, were considered (refer to Fig.16(a)).
因此，考虑了两种终止面情况，BaO和TiO2（参见图16(a)）。

Previous investigation suggests the prohibition of out-of-plane polarization in freestanding 1-uc thick BTO due to depolarization field.[75]
先前的研究表明，由于退极化场，在自支撑的1个单胞厚BaTiO3中禁止面外极化。[75]

Also, BTO with TiO2 termination has a large switching energy barrier, while the ferroelectric phase of BTO with BaO termination is less stable than the paraelectric phase.[75,76]
此外，TiO2终止面的BaTiO3具有较大的翻转能量势垒，而BaO终止面的BaTiO3的铁电相不如顺电相稳定。[75,76]

The 2D SnS layer is ferroelectric with robust in-plane polarization.
二维SnS层是铁电的，具有稳健的面内极化。

Due to a small lattice mismatch between the two layers in the BTO/SnS heterostructure, an in-plane strain to the BTO layer leads to overall in-plane polarization of the heterostructure.
由于BaTiO3/SnS异质结构中两层之间的晶格失配较小，对BaTiO3层施加的面内应变导致异质结构整体产生面内极化。

For the BaO (TiO2) terminated BTO/ SnS heterostructure, four (three) polar states were identified (see Fig. 16(c-f)).
对于BaO（TiO2）终止面的BaTiO3/SnS异质结构，确定了四个（三个）极性态（见图16(c-f)）。

The polarization changes directions between x and y, resulting in simultaneous ferroelastic transitions owing to the anisotropic strain induced at the interface.
极化在x和y方向之间改变方向，由于界面处诱导的各向异性应变，导致同时发生铁弹转变。

Fig. 16: (a) Geometric structures of 1-uc freestanding BTO with BaO (upper panel) and TiO2 (lower panel) terminations and (b) SnS monolayer.
图16: (a) 具有BaO（上图）和TiO2（下图）终止面的1个单胞厚自支撑BaTiO3的几何结构，以及(b) SnS单层。

Polar structures of 1-uc BTO on SnS with (c) BaO termination and (e) TiO2 termination.
(c) BaO终止面和(e) TiO2终止面的单胞厚BaTiO3在SnS上的极性结构。

(d, f) Energy barriers and polarization switching pathways for the BTO/SnS heterostructure with BaO termination and TiO2 termination, respectively.
(d, f) 分别为BaO终止面和TiO2终止面的BaTiO3/SnS异质结构的能量势垒和极化翻转路径。

Red and green arrows represent the polarization direction in the SnS and BTO layers.
红色和绿色箭头表示SnS和BaTiO3层中的极化方向。

Energy landscapes (left panels), polar state distributions of a BTO/SnS heterostructures (right panels), and polar state distributions of BTO (middle panels) as functions of the in-plane shift, for different thicknesses of SnS: (g-i) monolayer, (j-l) bilayer, and (m-o) trilayer.
不同SnS厚度下，作为面内位移函数的能量景观（左图）、BaTiO3/SnS异质结构的极性态分布（右图）以及BaTiO3的极性态分布（中图）：(g-i) 单层，(j-l) 双层，(m-o) 三层。

Sx and Sy are the displacements in the x- and y-directions, respectively.
Sx和Sy分别是x和y方向的位移。

(p, r) Schematic diagrams illustrating the joint twisting and sliding processes for heterobilayer and heterotrilayer systems, respectively.
(p, r) 分别说明异质双层和异质三层体系的联合扭转和滑动过程的示意图。

Tunable moiré patterns and ribboned domain patterns for twisted BTO on (q) monolayer and (s) bilayer SnS, respectively, achieved by interlayer sliding along the x-axis.
通过沿x轴层间滑动实现的转角BaTiO3在(q)单层和(s)双层SnS上的可调莫尔图案和带状畴图案。

The displacements tx and ty are represented in units being a/θ and b/θ, respectively.
位移tx和ty分别以a/θ和b/θ为单位表示。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [74]. Copyright © 2024 American Chemical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[74]。版权所有 © 2024 美国化学会。

The landau-like Ising model was employed to explore the dipole-dipole interactions, further confirming the ferroelectric nature with large and positive intralayer J values for the BTO and SnS.[74]
采用类Landau-Ising模型来探索偶极-偶极相互作用，进一步证实了BaTiO3和SnS具有大且正的层内J值的铁电特性。[74]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Ising model**</span>: 伊辛模型，一种描述相变、特别是铁磁性的简化统计物理模型，这里用于描述偶极子的有序排列。

While sliding, the total J coupling parameter is maximum for the ferroelectric states, reaching a minimum near the easy polarization reversal (paraelectric equivalent) state, hinting at the competitive behavior of the intralayer and interlayer dipole-dipole couplings.
在滑动时，总的J耦合参数在铁电态最大，在易极化反转（等效顺电态）附近达到最小值，暗示了层内和层间偶极-偶极耦合的竞争行为。

Since the polarization vector exhibits various directions, a 10 × 10 grid in one unit cell of the heterostructure, showing the polar nature with the lateral rigid shifts, was mapped (refer to Fig. 16(g-i)).
由于极化矢量表现出各种方向，绘制了异质结构一个晶胞内10×10的网格，显示了伴随横向刚性位移的极性特征（参见图16(g-i)）。

The polar state distributions show a dynamic topological nature for the BTO and BTO/SnS heterobilayer, converging at a Néel point with the winding number one, calculated for the square red box (see Fig. 16(h) &amp; (i)).
极性态分布显示出BaTiO3和BaTiO3/SnS异质双层的动态拓扑性质，它们汇聚在一个具有绕数为一的奈尔点，该绕数是为红色方框计算的（见图16(h)和(i)）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Néel point**</span>: 奈尔点，反铁磁材料的相变温度，类比于铁磁材料的居里点。此处指极化矢量场中的一个拓扑缺陷点。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Winding number**</span>: 绕数，一个描述矢量场围绕某一点旋转次数的拓扑不变量。

The distinct polar nature is lost in the BTO on the SnS bilayer heterostructure and for both BTO and BTO on the SnS trilayer heterostructure (refer to Fig. 16(k), (l), (n) &amp; (o)).
在SnS双层异质结构上的BaTiO3以及SnS三层异质结构上的BaTiO3和BaTiO3本身中，失去了明显的极性特征（参见图16(k), (l), (n)和(o)）。

Further, the polar state distributions have vectors curling around the moiré domain centers in the clockwise and counterclockwise Bloch vortexes.
此外，极性态分布的矢量在顺时针和逆时针的Bloch涡旋中，围绕莫尔畴中心旋转。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Bloch vortexes**</span>: 布洛赫涡旋，一种极化矢量呈涡旋状排列的拓扑结构，其极化方向在畴壁处连续变化。

The vortexes shift with the sliding of BTO and change nature with the SnS thickness, suggesting a way of their tunability (see Fig. 16(p-s)).
这些涡旋随着BaTiO3的滑动而移动，并随着SnS的厚度改变性质，这为它们的可调性提供了一种途径（见图16(p-s)）。

<span class="hljs-section"># 2.12 Quasi-1D NbI4</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 2.12 准一维 NbI4</span>

Though the title states “sliding ferroelectricity in 2D materials”, this subsection briefly discusses the sliding ferroelectricity in a quasi-1D material – NbI4, as investigated by Ding et al. in a recent publication.[77]
尽管标题为“二维材料中的滑动铁电性”，但本小节简要讨论了Ding等人在最近发表的文章中研究的一种准一维材料——NbI4中的滑动铁电性。[77]

Ferroelectricity in quasi-1D systems has been reported to enhance the ferroelectric memory density.[78]
据报道，准一维体系中的铁电性可以提高铁电存储密度。[78]

NbI4 bulk is a quasi-1D material with chains of atoms interacting with van der Waals forces.
NbI4块体是一种准一维材料，原子链通过范德华力相互作用。

The structure belongs to Cmc21 space group with every Nb atom surrounded by six I atoms.
该结构属于Cmc21空间群，每个Nb原子被六个I原子包围。

The structure exhibits Peierls dimerization, and the sliding of two chains induces polarization perpendicular to the sliding direction, in this case, only in the z-direction due to the geometry.
该结构表现出Peierls二聚化，两条链的滑动会诱导出垂直于滑动方向的极化，在这种情况下，由于几何形状的限制，仅在z方向上产生极化。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Peierls dimerization**</span>: Peierls二聚化，一维晶体中，原子发生周期性位移形成二聚体，导致体系能量降低的现象。

Three sliding paths were explored: perpendicular to the chain, along the chain, and intrachain distortion (altering the Peierls distortion in half of the chains).
探索了三种滑动路径：垂直于链、沿链方向和链内畸变（改变一半链中的Peierls畸变）。

For the first scenario, the polarization change is not monotonic, with the highest energy barrier of ~295 meV/u.c., while the lowest energy barrier of ~100 meV/u.c. is required for the switching of ferroelectric polarization of 0.11 μC/cm2, when the sliding motion is along the chain.
对于第一种情况，极化变化是非单调的，最高能量势垒约为295 meV/u.c.，而当滑动运动沿链方向时，翻转0.11 μC/cm^2的铁电极化所需的最低能量势垒约为100 meV/u.c.。

In addition, while the ground state is non-magnetic, the material was reported to transform from the non-magnetic ferroelectric to the non-magnetic polar metal, to the nonpolar antiferromagnetic metal by applying pressure to the system.
此外，虽然基态是非磁性的，但据报道，通过对体系施加压力，该材料会从非磁性铁电体转变为非磁性极性金属，再到非极性反铁磁金属。

<span class="hljs-section"># 3. Multiferroic vdW systems</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 3. 多铁性范德华体系</span>

This section, organized by materials, presents computationally predicted systems that exhibit coexisting sliding ferroelectricity and magnetism.
本节按材料分类，介绍了计算预测的展现出滑动铁电性和磁性共存的体系。

It explores the interplay between sliding ferroelectricity, magnetic ordering, and topological characteristics, highlighting the emergent multifunctional behavior.
探讨了滑动铁电性、磁有序和拓扑特性之间的相互作用，突出了涌现的多功能行为。

Additionally, potential applications are discussed based on theoretical insights into representative material systems.
此外，基于对代表性材料体系的理论洞察，讨论了潜在的应用。

<span class="hljs-section"># 3.1 3R VS2 bilayer</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 3.1 3R VS2 双层</span>

Fig. 17: (a, b) Top view of the VS2 bilayer in up (AB) and down (BA) polarization states.
图17: (a, b) 处于向上(AB)和向下(BA)极化态的VS2双层俯视图。

(c, d, e) Charge density difference plots for the VS2 bilayer in polar states (AB, BA) and the non-polar state (AA).
(c, d, e) 极性态(AB, BA)和非极性态(AA)中VS2双层的电荷密度差图。

(f) Double-well potential curve for the VSe2 bilayer (paramagnetic state) with respect to interlayer sliding.
(f) VSe2双层（顺磁态）关于层间滑动的双阱势曲线。

(g, h) Band structure with SOC for the antiferromagnetic FE configurations P↑M↑↓ and P↓M↓↑ and corresponding berry curvature in the first Brillouin zone.
(g, h) 反铁磁铁电构型P↑M↑↓和P↓M↓↑的SOC能带结构及第一布里渊区中的相应贝里曲率。

(i, j) Band structure with SOC for the antiferromagnetic FE configurations P↓M↑↓ and P↑M↓↑ and corresponding berry curvature in the first Brillouin zone.
(i, j) 反铁磁铁电构型P↓M↑↓和P↑M↓↑的SOC能带结构及第一布里渊区中的相应贝里曲率。

Variation of berry curvature at the (k) K− and (l) K+ points in the top valence band for the P↓M↑↓ and P↑M↓↑ configurations under different electric field intensity.
在不同电场强度下，P↓M↑↓和P↑M↓↑构型在顶级价带(k) K^-和(l) K^+点处的贝里曲率变化。

Dependence of berry curvature at the (m) K− and (n) K+ points in the highest valence band for the P↑M↑↓ and P↓M↓↑ configurations, under varying electric field intensity.
在不同电场强度下，P↑M↑↓和P↓M↓↑构型在最高价带(m) K^-和(n) K^+点处的贝里曲率依赖性。

(o) VS2 bilayer model illustrating the interlayer distance d.
(o) 说明层间距d的VS2双层模型。

(p) Variation of the magnetic moment (without SOC) as a function of interlayer distance variation and electric field intensity for the P↑M↑↓ and P↓M↓↑ configurations at (q) δd = 0. 6 Å and (r) δd = 0. 8 Å.
(p) 在(q) δd = 0.6 Å和(r) δd = 0.8 Å时，P↑M↑↓和P↓M↓↑构型的磁矩（无SOC）随层间距变化和电场强度变化的函数。

(s) Schematic representation of multistate control in the multiferroic VS2 bilayer.
(s) 多铁性VS2双层中多态控制的示意图。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [79]. Copyright © 2020 American Physical Society. Reprinted from ref. [80].
经许可重印自参考文献[79]。版权所有 © 2020 美国物理学会。重印自参考文献[80]。

Vanadium dichalcogenides (VX2; X = S, Se, Te) are another series of materials reported to exhibit sliding ferroelectricity [79,80], similar to 3R-MoS2, as discussed in section 2.4.
钒的二硫族化物(VX2; X = S, Se, Te)是另一系列报道显示滑动铁电性的材料[79,80]，与第2.4节讨论的3R-MoS2类似。

Though the most stable 2H bilayer stacking is inversion symmetric, 3R stacking (a stable phase) breaks inversion symmetry, allowing ferroelectric polarization to exist.
尽管最稳定的2H双层堆垛是反演对称的，但3R堆垛（一个稳定相）破坏了反演对称性，允许铁电极化存在。

Fig. 17(a) &amp; (b) show the top view and side views of the 3R stacked VS2 bilayer for the AB and BA polar states.
图17(a)和(b)显示了AB和BA极性态下3R堆垛VS2双层的俯视图和侧视图。

The polar AB and BA states belong to the P3m1 space group and are obtained by a lateral shift of [1/3, 2/3, 0] and [2/3, 1/3, 0] from the non-polar AA state, having P̅6m2 space group symmetry.
极性AB和BA态属于P3m1空间群，是通过从具有P̅6m2空间群对称性的非极性AA态分别横向移动[1/3, 2/3, 0]和[2/3, 1/3, 0]得到的。

The differential change density plots in Fig. 17(c), (d), and (e) unveil the zero and net interlayer dipole moment for the AA and AB (or BA) stacking, respectively.
图17(c)、(d)和(e)中的差分电荷密度图分别揭示了AA和AB（或BA）堆垛的零层间偶极矩和净层间偶极矩。

The nudged-elastic band calculation revealed an energy barrier of 19 [79] (4.88 [80]) meV, while one layer is dragged diagonally with respect to the other.
微动弹性带计算揭示了当一个层相对于另一个层沿对角线拖动时，能量势垒为19 [79] (4.88 [80]) meV。

Fig. 17(f) displays the familiar double well energy curve for the VSe2 bilayer for the paramagnetic phase.
图17(f)显示了顺磁相VSe2双层熟悉的双阱能量曲线。

As visible, the state AB switches to BA via an intermediate state (ST) as crossing an energy barrier of 9.9 meV.
如图所示，状态AB通过一个中间态(ST)并跨越9.9 meV的能量势垒翻转到BA。

Note that AB stacking is energetically favorable to AA stacking, and AA stacking is different from the intermediate state (ST), in fact, ST stacking is laterally shifted by [1/2, 1/2, 0] with respect to AA stacking (refer to Fig. 17(f)).
请注意，AB堆垛在能量上比AA堆垛更有利，并且AA堆垛与中间态(ST)不同，实际上，ST堆垛是相对于AA堆垛横向移动了[1/2, 1/2, 0]（参见图17(f)）。

Additionally, VX2 possesses magnetic moment and spontaneous valley polarization.
此外，VX2具有磁矩和自发谷极化。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Valley polarization**</span>: 谷极化，指在动量空间中不同谷的电子占据数不平衡，导致谷自由度的极化。

The monolayers are ferromagnetic, but for the bilayer, the configuration with opposite magnetic moments in the two layers possesses lowest energy, hence it is antiferromagnetic (AFM).[79]
单层是铁磁性的，但对于双层，两层中磁矩相反的构型能量最低，因此它是反铁磁的。[79]

Thus, four different multiferroic configurations considered are: P↑M↑↓, P↑M↓↑, P↓M↑↓, and P↓M↓↑.
因此，考虑了四种不同的多铁性构型：P↑M↑↓, P↑M↓↑, P↓M↑↓, 和 P↓M↓↑。

All these four configurations are found to be degenerate in energy.
发现所有这些四种构型在能量上是简并的。

The band structures and berry curvature of P↑M↑↓ and P↓M↓↑ configurations and P↓M↑↓ and P↑M↓↑ configurations (refer to Fig. 17(g-j)) are identical.
P↑M↑↓和P↓M↓↑构型以及P↓M↑↓和P↑M↓↑构型的能带结构和贝里曲率（参见图17(g-j)）是相同的。

Thus, the flipping of FE polarization requires the corresponding change in the magnetization of individual layers.
因此，铁电极化的翻转需要各层磁化强度发生相应的变化。

Further, the dependence of electric field intensity on the berry curvature (BC) distribution has been investigated (see Fig. 17).
此外，还研究了电场强度对贝里曲率分布的影响（见图17）。

The variation of BCs trend is again similar for P↑M↑↓ and P↓M↓↑ configurations, while irrelevant for P↑M↑↓ and P↑M↓↑ configurations (refer to Fig. 17(k-n)).
贝里曲率的变化趋势对于P↑M↑↓和P↓M↓↑构型再次相似，而与P↑M↑↓和P↑M↓↑构型无关（参见图17(k-n)）。

Thus, P↑M↑↓ and P↓M↓↑ configurations are FE bi-stable states, and P↓M↑↓ and P↑M↓↑ are ferroelectric switchable.
因此，P↑M↑↓和P↓M↓↑构型是铁电双稳态，而P↓M↑↓和P↑M↓↑是可铁电翻转的。

The FE control of magnetism confirms their coupling via ferrovalley in VS2 bilayer.
铁电对磁性的控制证实了它们在VS2双层中通过铁谷的耦合。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Ferrovalley**</span>: 铁谷，一种类似于铁电或铁磁的概念，指谷极化自发存在且可被外场翻转的物理状态。

Further, the AFM structure can be switched by application of magnetic field.
此外，反铁磁结构可以通过施加磁场来切换。

Thus, by applying electric and magnetic field, four states control of multiferroic system is possible (refer to Fig. 17(s)).
因此，通过施加电场和磁场，可以实现对多铁性体系的四态控制（参见图17(s)）。

Now, the interlayer distance change δd is defined as δd = d0 − d, where d0 is the equilibrium interlayer distance and d is the layer separation.
现在，层间距变化δd定义为δd = d<span class="hljs-emphasis">_0 - d，其中d_</span>0是平衡层间距，d是层分离。

The AFM to FM transition was also reported with variation in interlayer distance (see Fig. 17(p)), with system electronic transformation from semiconductor to metal.
还报道了随着层间距的变化，反铁磁到铁磁的转变（见图17(p)），体系的电子特性从半导体转变为金属。

Also, the variation of magnetic moment with the electric field has been investigated.
此外，还研究了磁矩随电场的变化。

The magnetization change is related to the electric field intensity by expression: μ0∆M = βSE2 + αSE, where αS and βS are the first and second order magnetoelectric coefficient.
磁化强度的变化与电场强度的关系由表达式 μ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_0∆M = β_</span>S E^2 + α<span class="hljs-emphasis">_S E 给出，其中α_</span>S和β<span class="hljs-emphasis">_S是一阶和二阶磁电系数。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Magnetoelectric coefficient**</span>: 磁电系数，描述材料中磁化强度与电场（或电极化与磁场）之间线性或非线性耦合的物理量。

For δd = 0.6 Å, magnetic moment for the VS2 bilayer displays a linear dependence on the applied electric field intensity, with αS ≈ −9.8 × 10−14 Gcm2V (−1.48 × 10−7s/m) for the P↓M↓↑ configuration (see Fig. 17(q)).
对于δd = 0.6 Å，VS2双层的磁矩显示出对外加电场强度的线性依赖关系，对于P↓M↓↑构型，α_</span>S ≈ −9.8 × 10^−14 Gcm^2V (−1.48 × 10^−7 s/m)（见图17(q)）。

In case of P↑M↓↑ configuration, the magnitude of αS is same with opposite sign.
对于P↑M↓↑构型，α<span class="hljs-emphasis">_S的大小相同，符号相反。

While for δd = 0.8 Å, VS2 bilayer exhibits second order magnetoelectric effect with βS ≈ −4.5 × 10−22 Gcm3V2 (−6.98 × 10−14s/V) and αS ≈ −4.8 × 10−14 Gcm2V (−7.372 × 10−8s/m) for the P↓M↓↑ configuration (refer to Fig. 17(r)).
而对于δd = 0.8 Å，VS2双层表现出二阶磁电效应，对于P↓M↓↑构型，β_</span>S ≈ −4.5 × 10^−22 Gcm^3V^2 (−6.98 × 10^−14 s/V) 和 α<span class="hljs-emphasis">_S ≈ −4.8 × 10^−14 Gcm^2V (−7.372 × 10^−8 s/m)（参见图17(r)）。

# 3.2 ZrI2
# 3.2 ZrI2

Zirconium diiodide (ZrI2) has also been explored in the context of sliding ferroelectricity.
二碘化锆(ZrI2)也在滑动铁电性的背景下被探索过。

The layered ZrI2 exists in three polymorph forms: 1T′or α (P21/m), Td or β (Pmn21), and γ (R̅3).[81–83]
层状ZrI2存在三种多晶型形式：1T′或α (P21/m), Td或β (Pmn21), 和 γ (R̅3)。[81–83]

Among the three, only Td or β has a polar structure and, thus, has the potential to exhibit ferroelectricity.
其中，只有Td或β具有极性结构，因此有可能表现出铁电性。

Ma et al. investigated the layered Td or β-ZrI2 for the sliding ferroelectricity and the formation of charged domain walls, while Zhang et al. explored the coupling between sliding ferroelectricity and ferroelasticity in the ZrI2 bilayer. [84,85]
Ma等人研究了层状Td或β-ZrI2的滑动铁电性和带电畴壁的形成，而Zhang等人则探索了ZrI2双层中滑动铁电性和铁弹性之间的耦合。[84,85]

The β-ZrI2 phase is isostructural to the 1T′-MoTe2 and Td-WTe2 (section 2.4).[86,87]
β-ZrI2相与1T′-MoTe2和Td-WTe2（第2.4节）同构。[86,87]

Fig. 18(a) depicts the crystal structures of different phases of ZrI2.
图18(a)描绘了ZrI2不同相的晶体结构。

The α and β phases of ZrI2 can be derived from the more symmetric T0 phase by distorting the structure via a − c angle or sliding the adjacent layers, respectively.
ZrI2的α和β相可以通过a-c角畸变结构或滑动相邻层，分别从对称性更高的T0相衍生出来。

The phonon spectrum of T0-ZrI2 features two imaginary modes: an optical zone centered mode (Γ2−) and a linear phonon mode (Γ4+).
T0-ZrI2的声子谱具有两个虚频模式：一个光学波区中心模式(Γ2−)和一个线性声子模式(Γ4+)。

From group theory analysis, it was revealed that these lattice instability modes transform the ZrI2 from T0 phase to Td or 1T′ phase (refer to Fig. 18(a) &amp; (b)).
通过群论分析，揭示了这些晶格不稳定性模式将ZrI2从T0相转变为Td或1T′相（参见图18(a)和(b)）。

In the Td phase, inversion symmetry is broken, with atoms in one layer displaced in the opposite direction to the atoms in the third layer.
在Td相中，反演对称性被破坏，一层中的原子位移方向与第三层中的原子相反。

Thus, the Td or β phase of bulk ZrI2 is ferroelectric with Td↑ and Td↓ bistable states exhibiting polarization 0.24 μC.m-2 and a low energy barrier of 5 meV (see Fig. 18(c) &amp; (d)).
因此，块体ZrI2的Td或β相是铁电的，具有Td↑和Td↓双稳态，表现出0.24 μC·m^-2的极化和5 meV的低能量势垒（见图18(c)和(d)）。

Meanwhile, the monolayer of the β-ZrI2 bulk possesses the space group of P21/m (C2h or MaC2a), and has been predicted to be a tristate ferroelastic (O1, O2, O3) switchable with the external strain.[85]
同时，β-ZrI2块体的单层具有P21/m（C2h或MaC2a）空间群，并已被预测为可通过外部应变翻转的三态铁弹体(O1, O2, O3)。[85]

With the bilayer stacking, the twofold screw rotation symmetry (C2a) is broken, reducing the space group symmetry to Pm (Cs).
随着双层堆垛，双重螺旋旋转对称性(C2a)被破坏，将空间群对称性降低到Pm(Cs)。

This results in broken inversion symmetry in bilayer stacking; hence, the ferroelectricity is feasible, alongside ferroelasticity.
这导致双层堆垛中的反演对称性破缺；因此，铁电性与铁弹性共存是可行的。

In contrast to bulk ZrI2, the reduced symmetry in the bilayer system allows the existence of out-of-plane polarization (2.1 × 10−4 C/m2) in addition to in-plane polarization (9.4 × 10−4 C/m2).
与块体ZrI2相比，双层体系中降低的对称性允许面外极化(2.1 × 10^−4 C/m^2)和面内极化(9.4 × 10^−4 C/m^2)同时存在。

Fig. 18(e) demonstrates the most bistable FE states and the intermediate state, with a switching energy barrier of 1.6 meV/f.u. for out-of-plane FE polarization (see Fig. 18(f)).
图18(e)展示了最稳定的铁电双稳态和中间态，面外铁电极化的翻转能量势垒为1.6 meV/f.u.（见图18(f)）。

Further, the isolated barrier of 0.06 meV/f.u. was obtained by simulating an opposite translation in one-unit cell in a 7×7 supercell.
此外，通过在7×7超胞中模拟一个晶胞内的相反平移，获得了0.06 meV/f.u.的孤立势垒。

The corresponding Curie temperature was estimated to be 476 K.
相应的居里温度估计为476 K。

Thus, considering the simultaneous existence of tristate ferroelasticity and bistate ferroelectricity, a six-logic-state multiferroicity was proposed: (O1, P±c); (O2, P±c); (O3, P±c).
因此，考虑到三态铁弹性和双态铁电性的同时存在，提出了一种六种逻辑态的多铁性：(O1, P±c); (O2, P±c); (O3, P±c)。

Although the in-plane polarization is not switchable via interlayer sliding, in-plane ferroelectricity and ferroelasticity are coupled to each other in the ZrI2 bilayer.
尽管面内极化不能通过层间滑动翻转，但在ZrI2双层中，面内铁电性和铁弹性是相互耦合的。

Thus, with ferroelastic switching, there is a simultaneous 120-degree change in the direction of in-plane polarization.
因此，随着铁弹翻转，面内极化方向同时发生120度的变化。

Additionally, by switching the out-of-plane polarization, in-plane spin textures are predicted to reverse, similar to as observed in Td-WTe2 bilayer (section 2.2).[11,85]
此外，通过翻转面外极化，预测面内自旋织构会发生反转，类似于在Td-WTe2双层中观察到的现象（第2.2节）。[11,85]

Fig. 18: (a) Crystal structures of the three phases of layered ZrI2: 1T′(α), T0, and Td(β).
图18: (a) 层状ZrI2的三个相的晶体结构：1T′(α), T0, 和 Td(β)。

The symbols M and P stand for the clockwise and counterclockwise rotated ZrI6 octahedra in ZrI2 bulk.
符号M和P代表ZrI2块体中顺时针和逆时针旋转的ZrI6八面体。

The ± sign represents the displacement direction of the I-I bond relative to the lower trilayer.
±符号表示I-I键相对于下层三层的位移方向。

(b) Enlarged view of the phonon spectrum for T0- ZrI2.
(b) T0-ZrI2声子谱的放大视图。

(c) FE polarization in Td(β)-ZrI2 via the intermediate T0 phase.
(c) Td(β)-ZrI2中通过中间T0相的铁电极化。

(d) Double-well energy curve and polarization as a function of interlayer sliding distance for Td(β)- ZrI2, with origin corresponding to T0 phase.
(d) Td(β)-ZrI2的双阱能量曲线和极化作为层间滑动距离的函数，原点对应于T0相。

(e) FE polarization states of β-ZrI2 bilayer and (f) corresponding NEB employed energy barrier for FE switching.
(e) β-ZrI2双层的铁电极化态和(f) 用于铁电翻转的相应NEB能量势垒。

(g) First-principles-calculated domain walls (DWs) for ZrI2 bulk within a 1×1×8 supercell, showing tail-to-tail (TT) and head-to-head (HH) DWs arrangements in the Td/Td configuration.
(g) 在1×1×8超胞内，通过第一性原理计算的ZrI2块体的畴壁，显示了Td/Td构型中尾对尾和头对头的畴壁排列。

(h) Band structure and charge density distributions at the valence band maximum (VBM) and conduction band minimum (CBM1 and CBM2).
(h) 价带顶和导带底（CBM1和CBM2）处的能带结构和电荷密度分布。

(i) Planar and macroscopic average potentials across the supercell, alongside planar average charge densities at the VBM, CBM1, and CBM2.
(i) 超胞上的平面和宏观平均电势，以及价带顶、CBM1和CBM2处的平面平均电荷密度。

Reprinted from ref. [84]. Reprinted with permission from ref. [85]. Copyright © 2021 American Physical Society.
重印自参考文献[84]。经许可重印自参考文献[85]。版权所有 © 2021 美国物理学会。

Next, the domain wall width, energy barrier, and charge have been reported to significantly influence device performance from the polarization to the carrier mobility.[88]
接下来，据报道，畴壁宽度、能量势垒和电荷会显著影响器件性能，从极化到载流子迁移率。[88]

Thus, Ma et al. investigated the different domain structures in the bulk FE ZrI2.[84]
因此，Ma等人研究了块体铁电ZrI2中的不同畴结构。[84]

It was suggested that since the 1T′ and Td phases have a small energy difference (6 meV/u.c.), both phases can exist in the domain wall structures.
据指出，由于1T′和Td相的能量差很小（6 meV/u.c.），两种相都可以存在于畴壁结构中。

Two types of domain walls (DWs), polar and non-polar were discussed for the 1T′/1T′ and Td/Td arrangements, respectively.
讨论了两种类型的畴壁，分别对应1T′/1T′和Td/Td排列的极性和非极性畴壁。

In the 1T′/1T′ arrangement, Td pattern is formed at the boundary.
在1T′/1T′排列中，在边界处形成了Td图案。

Also, since the 1T′structure is non-polar, there are no boundary charges but rather only a ferroelectric interface.
此外，由于1T′结构是非极性的，没有边界电荷，而只有一个铁电界面。

The DWs for the Td/Td arrangement comprise the head-to-head (HH) 1T′ boundary at the center and tail-to-tail 1T′ boundary at the corners (see Fig. 18(g)).
Td/Td排列的畴壁包括中心的头对头1T′边界和角落的尾对尾1T′边界（见图18(g)）。

In this case, the presence of bound charges at the interfaces results in band bending with valence and conduction bands reaching Fermi level and the formation of a potential difference of 0.24 eV (see Fig. 18(h) &amp; (i)).
在这种情况下，界面处存在的束缚电荷导致能带弯曲，价带和导带到达费米能级，并形成0.24 eV的电势差（见图18(h)和(i)）。

Also, the HH DW exhibits a higher charge concentration than TT DW, as depicted in Fig. 18(i).
此外，如图18(i)所示，头对头畴壁比尾对尾畴壁表现出更高的电荷浓度。

The metallic character of DW can allow noticeably high carrier mobility.[89]
畴壁的金属特性可以允许非常高的载流子迁移率。[89]

Thus, it was suggested that modulation carrier mobility is feasible via interlayer sliding.
因此，表明通过层间滑动来调制载流子迁移率是可行的。

Further, the formation energy of 1 mJ.m-2 for the charged DW was estimated using the expression EDW = 2PelEg/e, where Pel is the electric polarization and Eg is the band gap.
此外，使用表达式 E_</span>DW = 2P<span class="hljs-emphasis">_el E_</span>g / e 估算了带电畴壁的形成能为1 mJ·m^-2，其中Pel是电极化，Eg是带隙。

Ding et al. explored the enhancement in polarization of layered ZrI2 with mechanical tuning. [90]
Ding等人探索了通过机械调谐来增强层状ZrI2的极化。[90]

Further, the dipole enhancement due to interlayer sliding ferroelectricity with applied vertical compression was reported to contribute significantly to the negative longitudinal piezoelectricity of the material.
此外，据报道，在施加垂直压缩的情况下，由于层间滑动铁电性导致的偶极增强，对材料的负纵向压电性有显著贡献。

<span class="hljs-section"># 3.3 MnBi2Te4</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 3.3 MnBi2Te4</span>

The 2H-stacked MnBi2Te4 (MBT) bilayer is another interesting material that exhibits sliding ferroelectricity.
2H堆垛的MnBi2Te4 (MBT)双层是另一种表现出滑动铁电性的有趣材料。

Apart from the sliding ferroelectricity, the material hosts magnetism as well as nontrivial topological phases.
除了滑动铁电性，该材料还具有磁性和非平凡的拓扑相。

Ren et al. proposed the tuning in the magnetism as well as topology with the interlayer sliding.[91]
Ren等人提出通过层间滑动来调节磁性和拓扑性质。[91]

This approach could allow a more practically feasible electric control of magnetism and topology in device applications.
这种方法可以在器件应用中实现更实际可行的对磁性和拓扑的电控。

Two stacking cases i.e., 3R and 2H with three stacking orders (AA, AB, AC) were investigated with AB stacked 3R-MBT being the ground state (refer to Fig. 19(a), (b) &amp; (d)).
研究了两种堆垛情况，即3R和2H，以及三种堆垛序（AA, AB, AC），其中AB堆垛的3R-MBT是基态（参见图19(a), (b)和(d)）。

The non-primed (primed) stacking orders correspond to the 3R (2H) stacking case.
非撇号（撇号）的堆垛序对应于3R (2H)堆垛情况。

Only the 2H-stacking case allows the existence of polarization due to broken inversion symmetry.
只有2H堆垛情况下，由于反演对称性破缺，才允许极化存在。

There are two metastable configurations (AB′ and AC′) for the 2H-stacking, having similar energies, lesser than AA and AC sequences.
对于2H堆垛，有两个亚稳态构型（AB′和AC′），具有相似的能量，低于AA和AC序列。

The NEB calculation suggested an energy barrier of 30 meV/u.c. for ferroelectric switching between the AB′ and AC′ states (see Fig. 19(c)).
NEB计算表明，AB′和AC′状态之间铁电翻转的能量势垒为30 meV/u.c.（见图19(c)）。

The differential charge density plots show the charge polarization for the AB′ and AC′, none for the BC′ configuration, further confirming the ferroelectric polarization switching between the two states (refer to insets Fig. 19(c)).
差分电荷密度图显示了AB′和AC′的电荷极化，而BC′构型没有，进一步证实了两种状态之间的铁电极化翻转（参见图19(c)插图）。

Fig. 19: (a) Side view of 3R and 2H stacking configurations for the bilayer MoBi2Te4.
图19: (a) 双层MoBi2Te4的3R和2H堆垛构型的侧视图。

(b) Top view of the AB stacking configuration, with a green arrow representing the sliding pathway.
(b) AB堆垛构型的俯视图，绿色箭头代表滑动路径。

Red (blue) balls are the Mn atoms in the upper (lower) layer, while the yellow (purple) balls correspond to Te (Bi) atoms.
红（蓝）球是上（下）层中的Mn原子，而黄（紫）球对应于Te（Bi）原子。

(c) Ferroelectric switching pathway for the 2H stacking configuration.
(c) 2H堆垛构型的铁电翻转路径。

Insets show the differential charge density at the minimum and maximum energy points along the pathway.
插图显示了沿路径最小和最大能量点处的差分电荷密度。

(d) Variation of total energy (with out-of-plane spin order) and (e) interlayer exchange energy along the sliding pathway.
(d) 沿滑动路径的总能量（具有面外自旋序）和(e) 层间交换能的变化。

(f) Projected band structures for different stackings under the out-of-plane FM order, with insets highlighting the edge states.
(f) 在面外铁磁序下，不同堆垛的投影能带结构，插图突出了边缘态。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [91]. Copyright © 2022 American Physical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[91]。版权所有 © 2022 美国物理学会。

Next, the magnetic orders of all configurations were also analyzed.
接下来，还分析了所有构型的磁序。

For this purpose, the exchange energy difference between out-of-plane FM and AFM orders was calculated, revealing the AFM order for all configurations except AA (see Fig. 19(e)).
为此，计算了面外铁磁与反铁磁序之间的交换能差，揭示了除AA外所有构型都呈反铁磁序（见图19(e)）。

Compared to ferromagnets, antiferromagnetic materials are superior due to the faster spin dynamics and their low sensitivity to stray magnetic fields and, thus, are far more suitable for spintronics applications.
与铁磁体相比，反铁磁材料具有更快的自旋动力学和对杂散磁场不敏感的优点，因此更适合于自旋电子学应用。

The FM stacking order (AA) was associated with comparatively weaker interlayer coupling and larger interlayer distance.
铁磁堆垛序（AA）与相对较弱的层间耦合和较大的层间距相关。

The magnetic anisotropy verified the preference for the out-of-plane direction.
磁各向异性证实了对面外方向的偏好。

Thus, the magnetic phase can be switched by interlayer sliding.
因此，磁相可以通过层间滑动来切换。

Further, the interlayer sliding was also observed to affect the band structure.
此外，还观察到层间滑动会影响能带结构。

It was reported that for some configurations (AB, AB′, AC′) in FM order, the band structure becomes non-trivial with the appearance of spin-polarized edge state and non-zero Chern number (see Fig. 19(f)).
据报道，对于某些处于铁磁序的构型（AB, AB′, AC′），能带结构随着自旋极化边缘态和非零陈数的出现而变得非平凡（见图19(f)）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Chern number**</span>: 陈数，一个用于表征二维拓扑绝缘体霍尔电导量子化的拓扑不变量。

As seen for the AB, AB′, and AC′ configurations, band inversion is observed around the Γ point.
正如对AB、AB′和AC′构型所看到的，在Γ点周围观察到能带反转。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Band inversion**</span>: 能带反转，指导带和价带在特定k点附近发生交换，是拓扑非平凡态的标志。

It is expected that such configurations may exhibit quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) effect.
预计这些构型可能会表现出量子反常霍尔效应。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) effect**</span>: 量子反常霍尔效应，指在没有外加磁场的情况下，由于材料自身的磁性和拓扑性质，产生量子化的霍尔电导。

Again, it was emphasized that interlayer coupling and distance play a major role.
再次强调了层间耦合和距离起着重要作用。

For the trivial insulators, the interlayer distance is larger with reduced interlayer coupling of Te-pz orbitals.
对于平凡绝缘体，层间距较大，Te-pz轨道的层间耦合减弱。

Thus, the interlayer sliding could also be useful in the quantum phase transition.
因此，层间滑动在量子相变中也可能有用。

<span class="hljs-section"># 3.3.1 Electric control of quantum anomalous Hall effect</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 3.3.1 量子反常霍尔效应的电控</span>

Fig. 20: (a) Crystal structure of bulk MnBi2Te4 (MBT).
图20: (a) 块体MnBi2Te4 (MBT)的晶体结构。

(b) Atomic structures of four-layer MnBi2Te4 (FL-MBT) after breaking P symmetry and corresponding ferroelectric switching.
(b) 打破P对称性后四层MnBi2Te4 (FL-MBT)的原子结构及相应的铁电翻转。

(c) NEB calculated energy barrier for P1-FLMBT.
(c) NEB计算的P1-FLMBT能量势垒。

(d) The band structure of P1-FLMBT with SOC.
(d) 具有SOC的P1-FLMBT能带结构。

(e) Anomalous Hall conductivity for the P1-FLMBT and P′1-FLMBT.
(e) P1-FLMBT和P′1-FLMBT的反常霍尔电导率。

(f) Chern number as a function of interlayer distance increment (δd) between surface single layer and trilayer in pristine FLMBT.
(f) 原始FLMBT中，表面单层与三层之间层间距增量(δd)的函数陈数。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [92]. Copyright © 2024 American Physical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[92]。版权所有 © 2024 美国物理学会。

Liang et al. investigated the sliding ferroelectricity along with the quantum anomalous Hall effect in 3R-stacked four-layer MnBi2Te4 (FLMBT).[92]
Liang等人研究了3R堆垛四层MnBi2Te4 (FLMBT)中的滑动铁电性以及量子反常霍尔效应。[92]

Due to P symmetry, electric polarization is not allowed in pristine FLMBT (see Fig. 20(a)).
由于P对称性，在原始FLMBT中不允许存在电极化（见图20(a)）。

So, the idea is to slightly slide the uppermost and lowermost layers in opposite directions to break inversion symmetry (or slide the P symmetric center bilayer) to obtain structures with opposite polarizations, P1 FLMBT and P1′-FLMBT (refer to Fig. 20(b)).
因此，其想法是使最上层和最下层沿相反方向轻微滑动，以打破反演对称性（或滑动P对称中心双层），从而获得具有相反极化的结构P1-FLMBT和P1′-FLMBT（参见图20(b)）。

As a result of the unequal environments faced by top and bottom layers, an out-of-plane polarization of 6.82 × 10-13 C/m was reported for P1 FLMBT.
由于顶层和底层面临的环境不等价，据报道P1-FLMBT的面外极化为6.82 × 10^-13 C/m。

The NEB calculation suggested an energy barrier of 70.26 meV/u.c. for the polarization switching (refer to Fig. 20(c)).
NEB计算表明极化翻转的能量势垒为70.26 meV/u.c.（参见图20(c)）。

The paraelectric PE1-FLMBT possesses no electric polarization due to its centrosymmetric nature.
顺电PE1-FLMBT由于其中心对称性而不具有电极化。

Next, the magnetic analysis predicted the AFM order is retained in the P1-FLMBT with the exchange of spin-up and down channels with the ferroelectric polarization in P1-FLMBT and P1′-FLMBT.
接下来，磁学分析预测，在P1-FLMBT和P1′-FLMBT中，随着铁电极化的翻转，自旋向上和向下的通道发生交换，但反铁磁序得以保持。

Since the symmetry and band structure are altered by interlayer sliding, the change in topology of the material was investigated.
由于层间滑动改变了对称性和能带结构，因此研究了材料拓扑结构的变化。

The calculation of Chern number defined as the integral of Berry curvature over Brillouin zone, (C = 1(-1) for P1(P1′)-FLMBT) revealed non trivial topology.
计算定义了贝里曲率在布里渊区上的积分即陈数（对于P1(P1′)-FLMBT，C = 1(-1)），揭示了非平凡拓扑。

The inference is verified by the anomalous Hall conductivity (σxy) reaching 1(-1) × e2/h for the P1(P1′)-FLMBT (refer to Fig. 20(e)).
这一推论通过P1(P1′)-FLMBT的反常霍尔电导率(σxy)达到1(-1) × e^2/h得到了验证（参见图20(e)）。

Thus, the AFM spin distribution, Berry curvature, and QAH σxy sign reverses with the reversal in the sign ferroelectric polarization.
因此，反铁磁自旋分布、贝里曲率和量子反常霍尔效应电导率的符号随着铁电极化符号的反转而反转。

The above inferences qualify the P1-FLMBT as a FE QAH insulator.
上述推论使P1-FLMBT符合作铁电量子反常霍尔绝缘体的条件。
<span class="hljs-strong">**FE QAH insulator**</span>: 铁电量子反常霍尔绝缘体，一种同时具有铁电性和量子反常霍尔效应的新型拓扑绝缘体。

This is contrary to the previous investigations suggesting the even layer MBT as a axion insulator.[93–95]
这与先前认为偶数层MBT是轴子绝缘体的研究相反。[93–95]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Axion insulator**</span>: 轴子绝缘体，一种拓扑绝缘体，其表面态被磁性打开能隙，但体内仍保持拓扑非平庸性质。

It is worthy to note that the sliding-manipulated ferroelectric states such as P1-FLMBT are less stable than their pristine structure.
值得注意的是，通过滑动操控的铁电态（如P1-FLMBT）的稳定性不如其原始结构。

Thus, it was suggested rather to employ a small angular twist between surface layer and remaining layers, to obtain large domains with enhanced stability in real systems.
因此，建议在表面层和其余层之间采用小角度扭转，以在实际体系中获得具有增强稳定性的大面积畴。

<span class="hljs-section"># 3.4 CrI3, Cr2Ge2Te6, and Fe3GeTe2 bilayers</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 3.4 CrI3, Cr2Ge2Te6, 和 Fe3GeTe2 双层</span>

Fig. 21: Geometric structures of (a) anti-parallel stacked CrI3 bilayer, (b) anti-parallel stacked Cr2Ge2Te6 bilayer, and (c) parallel stacked Fe3GeTe2 bilayer.
图21: (a) 反平行堆垛的CrI3双层，(b) 反平行堆垛的Cr2Ge2Te6双层，以及(c) 平行堆垛的Fe3GeTe2双层的几何结构。

The states I and II in (a), (b) &amp; (c) are bi-stable ferroelectric states with opposite polarization marked as red arrows.
(a), (b)和(c)中的状态I和II是具有相反极化的双稳态铁电态，如红色箭头所示。

(d) The energy barrier for the ferroelectric switching for the CrI3, Cr2Ge2Te6, and Fe3GeTe2 bilayers.
(d) CrI3, Cr2Ge2Te6, 和 Fe3GeTe2 双层铁电翻转的能量势垒。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [96]. Copyright © 2023 Elsevier.
经许可重印自参考文献[96]。版权所有 © 2023 Elsevier。

Zhong et al. investigated sliding ferroelectricity in the prevalent magnetic materials: CrI3, Cr2Ge2Te6, and Fe3GeTe2.[96–98]
Zhong等人研究了常见磁性材料中的滑动铁电性：CrI3, Cr2Ge2Te6, 和 Fe3GeTe2。[96–98]

The CrI3 bilayer is ferromagnetic, while the Cr2Ge2Te6 and Fe3GeTe2 bilayers are anti-ferromagnetic in their ground state.
CrI3双层是铁磁性的，而Cr2Ge2Te6和Fe3GeTe2双层在其基态是反铁磁性的。

For the CrI3 and Cr2Ge2Te6 bilayers, the parallel stacking is centrosymmetric, while the anti-parallel stacking is non-centrosymmetric (see Fig. 21(a) &amp; (b)).
对于CrI3和Cr2Ge2Te6双层，平行堆垛是中心对称的，而反平行堆垛是非中心对称的（见图21(a)和(b)）。

On the other hand, even parallel-stacked Fe3GeTe2 bilayer is non-centrosymmetric (refer to Fig. 21(c)).
另一方面，即使是平行堆垛的Fe3GeTe2双层也是非中心对称的（参见图21(c)）。

The polarization and NEB calculated energy barrier (Fig. 21(d)) values are given in Table 1.
极化和NEB计算的能量势垒（图21(d)）值在表1中给出。

The spin-polarized band structure with Hubbard correction U revealed indirect band gap for the CrI3 and Cr2Ge2Te6 bilayers, while metallic nature for the Fe3GeTe2 bilayer.[96]
具有Hubbard U修正的自旋极化能带结构揭示了CrI3和Cr2Ge2Te6双层具有间接带隙，而Fe3GeTe2双层具有金属性。[96]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Hubbard correction U**</span>: 哈伯德修正，一种在密度泛函理论计算中，对强关联电子体系的库仑相互作用进行修正的方法。

Specifically, the total magnetic moment of 0.01 μB per unit cell is highlighted for the Fe3GeTe2 bilayer, which reverses with ferroelectric switching.
特别强调了Fe3GeTe2双层每晶胞0.01 μB的总磁矩，该磁矩会随铁电翻转而反转。

Further, the effect of reducing the interlayer distance on the polarization and net magnetization was investigated for the Fe3GeTe2 bilayer.
此外，针对Fe3GeTe2双层，研究了减小层间距对极化和净磁化强度的影响。

As discussed before, the polarization increases with the decrease in the interlayer distance.
如前所述，极化随层间距的减小而增加。

It was predicted that the polarization and net magnetization almost doubles with the compressive strain of ~10%, termed as “piezo multiferroic effect”.[96]
据预测，在大约10%的压缩应变下，极化和净磁化强度几乎翻倍，这被称为“压电多铁效应”。[96]

<span class="hljs-section"># 3.5 MnSe</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 3.5 MnSe</span>

Fig. 22: (a) The energy profile of interlayer sliding of MnSe bilayer.
图22: (a) MnSe双层间滑动的能量分布图。

(b) The energy barrier for the ferroelectricity switching AB→BA of MnSe bilayer.
(b) MnSe双层铁电翻转AB→BA的能量势垒。

(c) The geometric structures of the bi-stable ferroelectric states AB, BA and the intermediate state IS.
(c) 双稳态铁电态AB、BA和中间态IS的几何结构。

(d) The polarization and magnetic moment along the switching pathway AB→BA.
(d) 沿翻转路径AB→BA的极化和磁矩。

(e) The differential charge density with yellow (blue) bubbles denoting the accumulation (depletion) of electrons in bi-stable states.
(e) 双稳态中的差分电荷密度，黄色（蓝色）气泡表示电子的积累（耗尽）。

The variation in electric potential difference and magnetic moment with the application of (f) compressive out-of-plane strain and (g) biaxial in-plane strain for AB-stacked MnSe bilayer.
AB堆垛MnSe双层在施加(f) 压缩面外应变和(g) 双轴面内应变时，电势差和磁矩的变化。

(h) The effect of hole doping on the magnetic moment in AB and BA configurations.
(h) 空穴掺杂对AB和BA构型中磁矩的影响。

(i) The synergic effect of biaxial in-plane strain and hole doping on the magnetic moment of AB stacked MnSe bilayer.
(i) 双轴面内应变和空穴掺杂对AB堆垛MnSe双层磁矩的协同效应。

Reprinted from ref. [99].
重印自参考文献[99]。

Liu et al. investigated sliding ferroelectricity and the magnetoelectric effect in AFM MnSe materials.[99]
Liu等人研究了反铁磁MnSe材料中的滑动铁电性和磁电效应。[99]

The monolayer MnSe is centrosymmetric with two sublayers with opposite buckling and AFM intralayer coupling.
单层MnSe是中心对称的，具有两个褶皱方向相反的亚层和反铁磁层内耦合。

The two minimal degenerate energy stackings of MnSe bilayer are AB and BA (see Fig. 22(c)), belonging to the P3m1 space group.
MnSe双层的两个最小能量简并堆垛是AB和BA（见图22(c)），属于P3m1空间群。

The bilayer possesses out-of-plane polarization of 2.7 pC.m-1 (see Fig. 22(d)) with switching barrier of 8.4 meV (refer to Fig. 22(a) &amp; (b)) and 0.23 V electric potential difference between the layers.
该双层具有2.7 pC·m^-1的面外极化（见图22(d)），翻转势垒为8.4 meV（参见图22(a)和(b)），层间电势差为0.23 V。

The high value of polarization was attributed to stronger interlayer hybridization of Mn-dz2 and Se pz orbitals.
如此高的极化值归因于Mn-dz^2和Se-pz轨道的强层间杂化。

The magnetic force theory (MFT) employing Green’s function was utilized to obtain the magnetic exchange parameters and their orbital contributions.
利用基于格林函数的磁力理论获得了磁交换参数及其轨道贡献。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Magnetic force theory (MFT)**</span>: 磁力理论，一种基于第一性原理计算，用于求解海森堡模型中的磁交换耦合参数的方法。

The investigation suggested AFM coupling at the interface with dominant contribution from the Mn-dz2 and Se pz hybridized orbitals.
研究表明，界面处存在反铁磁耦合，其主要贡献来自Mn-dz^2和Se-pz杂化轨道。

Further, the electric polarization breaks the degeneracy of bands corresponding to opposite spins leading to electrically controlled Zeeman spin splitting and net magnetic moment of 8 mμB per cell in the system (refer to Fig. 22(d)).
此外，电极化打破了相反自旋对应能带的简并，导致了电控塞曼自旋劈裂和体系中每胞8 mμB的净磁矩（参见图22(d)）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Zeeman spin splitting**</span>: 塞曼自旋劈裂，指在磁场或电场作用下，自旋简并的能级发生分裂的现象。

Similar consequences were suggested for the ABA trilayer and ABAB tetralayer MnSe systems.
对于ABA三层和ABAB四层MnSe体系，也提出了类似的结果。

Their polarization and energy switching barriers are provided in Table 1.
它们的极化和能量翻转势垒在表1中提供。

The magnetoelectric effect was reported to be linear against the layer number with the magnetic moment reaching 16 mμB and 24 mμB for the trilayer and tetralayer systems.
据报道，磁电效应与层数呈线性关系，三层和四层体系的磁矩分别达到16 mμB和24 mμB。

The magnetic anisotropy analysis suggested in-plane preference to the out-of-plane direction.
磁各向异性分析表明，面内方向优于面外方向。

Next, the effect of strain and hole doping on the polarization and magnetic moment of MnSe bilayer was probed.
接下来，探讨了应变和空穴掺杂对MnSe双层极化和磁矩的影响。

It was predicted that the compressive out-of-plane strain, defined as σz = (d0 − d)/d0, where d (d0) is the (equilibrium) interlayer distance, enhances the electric potential difference (hence, polarization) and magnetic moment in the system (see Fig. 22(f)).
据预测，压缩面外应变（定义为σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_z = (d_</span>0 - d)/d<span class="hljs-emphasis">_0，其中d (d_</span>0)是（平衡）层间距）会增强体系中的电势差（从而增强极化）和磁矩（见图22(f)）。

It was demonstrated that the reason of this increase is the increased hybridization of Mn-dz2 and Se-pz of orbitals (a conclusion made from increased Zeeman spin splitting in the band structure with the strain).[99]
据证明，这种增加的原因是Mn-dz^2和Se-pz轨道的杂化增强（这一结论是从应变下能带结构中塞曼自旋劈裂的增强中得出的）。[99]

While the compressive (tensile) biaxial in-plane strain (σx/y = (a − a0)/a0) was found to suppress (enhance) the polarization and magnetic moment (refer to Fig. 22(g)).
而发现压缩（拉伸）双轴面内应变（σ<span class="hljs-emphasis">_x/y = (a - a0)/a0）会抑制（增强）极化和磁矩（参见图22(g)）。

Also, the hole doping affects the electronic and magnetic properties of a material.
此外，空穴掺杂会影响材料的电子和磁学性质。

It was reported that increase in hole doping concentration, for the AB-stacked MnSe bilayer, magnetic moment first becomes negative, then positive reaching a maximum of 0.3 μB for the hole doping density of 1.39 × 1015 cm-2 (see Fig. 22(h)).
据报道，对于AB堆垛的MnSe双层，随着空穴掺杂浓度的增加，磁矩首先变为负值，然后变为正值，在空穴掺杂密度为1.39 × 10^15 cm^-2时达到最大值0.3 μB（见图22(h)）。

The sign of magnetic moment remains opposite for the AB and BA configurations, throughout the variable hole doping density.
在空穴掺杂密度变化的过程中，AB和BA构型的磁矩符号始终保持相反。

Next, the synergic effects of tensile biaxial in-plane strain and hole doping on the magnetic moment of AB-stacked MnSe bilayer.
接下来，研究了拉伸双轴面内应变和空穴掺杂对AB堆垛MnSe双层磁矩的协同效应。

As visible in Fig. 22(i), with the increase in strain, the magnetic moment reaches a higher maximum for a lower hole doping concentration, making it more attainable in the experimental setup.
如图22(i)所示，随着应变的增加，磁矩在较低的空穴掺杂浓度下达到更高的最大值，这使得其在实验装置中更易于实现。

The structurally similar FeS system is also multiferroic with an electric polarization of 4.1 pC.m-1 with an energy barrier of 6.4 meV.
结构相似的FeS体系也是多铁性的，电极化为4.1 pC·m^-1，能量势垒为6.4 meV。

# 3.6 VI2
# 3.6 VI2

Yu et al. demonstrated ferroelectricity in VI2 monolayer arising from the in-plane spin spiral order along with spin-orbit coupling and, in addition, due to sliding ferroelectricity in VI2 multilayers.[100]
Yu等人证明了VI2单层中铁电性来源于面内自旋螺旋序和自旋轨道耦合，此外，VI2多层中的铁电性也源于滑动铁电性。[100]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Spin spiral order**</span>: 自旋螺旋序，一种非共线的磁有序结构，其自旋方向在空间中呈周期性旋转。

The polarization in multilayers is predicted to be one order higher than the monolayer.
多层的极化预计比单层高一个数量级。

The system hosts noncollinear AFM order with vector-spin spiral chirality (V) of ±1 and scalar-spin spiral chirality (S) of zero (non-zero for noncoplanar spin order) (see Fig. 23(b) &amp; (c)).
该体系具有非共线反铁磁序，其矢量自旋螺旋手性为±1，标量自旋螺旋手性为零（对于非共面自旋序则为非零）（见图23(b)和(c)）。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Spin spiral chirality**</span>: 自旋螺旋手性，描述自旋在空间中旋转方向（顺时针或逆时针）的性质。

The spin spiral order induces a non-displacive ferroelectricity in the system, which is expressed as:
自旋螺旋序在体系中诱导出非位移型铁电性，其表达式为：

P = M ∑ (Si × Sj)
<span class="language-xml"><span class="hljs-tag">&lt;<span class="hljs-name">ij</span>&gt;</span></span> (eq. 17)
P = M ∑_</span>{<span class="language-xml"><span class="hljs-tag">&lt;<span class="hljs-name">ij</span>&gt;</span></span>} (S<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i × S_</span>j) (eq. 17)

where S denotes the spin vector and M is a 3×3 coefficient matrix.
其中S表示自旋矢量，M是一个3×3的系数矩阵。

The summation runs over all the nearest neighbor bonds.
求和涵盖所有最近邻键。

The DFT calculations revealed an antisymmetric mechanism (only diagonal elements in the coefficient matrix) for the spin spiral-induced ferroelectricity with out-of-plane polarization of 0.12 pC.m-1.[100,101]
DFT计算揭示了自旋螺旋诱导铁电性的反对称机制（系数矩阵中只有对角元素），其面外极化为0.12 pC·m^-1。[100,101]

The direction of spin spiral chirality in the VI2 monolayer can be tuned by the application of an electric field (see Fig. 23(e)).
VI2单层中自旋螺旋手性的方向可以通过施加电场来调节（见图23(e)）。

Another way is to have an intrinsic electric field, realized here by layer stacking.
另一种方式是通过层堆叠实现内建电场。

The AB and AB′ stackings are energetically degenerate and exhibit opposite polarization of 1.33 pC.m-1 (without considering Y-AFM order).
AB和AB′堆垛在能量上是简并的，并表现出相反的极化1.33 pC·m^-1（不考虑Y-反铁磁序）。

The ferroelectric switching path through AC provides a lower barrier of 21.06 meV compared to 77.52 meV via AA configuration (refer to Fig. 23(f) &amp; (g)).
通过AC的铁电翻转路径提供了21.06 meV的较低势垒，而通过AA构型的势垒为77.52 meV（参见图23(f)和(g)）。

The AC configuration has non-zero polarization (0.18 pC.m-1) corresponding to the spin spiral induced ferroelectricity but zero ∆EDD→UU.
AC构型具有对应于自旋螺旋诱导铁电性的非零极化(0.18 pC·m^-1)，但∆E<span class="hljs-emphasis">_DD→UU为零。

The AB and AB′ configurations have the direction of spin spiral chirality in the noncollinear Y-AFM ground state aligned along the intrinsic electric field with ∆EDD→UU of 0.14 meV/unit cell.
AB和AB′构型在非共线Y-反铁磁基态中自旋螺旋手性的方向沿内建电场排列，其∆E_</span>DD→UU为0.14 meV/晶胞。

This was termed as “spin spiral chirality sliding ferroelectricity” locking effect.
这被称为“自旋螺旋手性-滑动铁电性”锁定效应。

Thus, the spin spiral chiral can be tuned by interlayer sliding without any external electric field.
因此，自旋螺旋手性可以通过层间滑动来调节，无需任何外部电场。

Also, the spin spiral order induced polarization and ∆EDD→UU increases linearly with the spin-orbit strength (λSOC) (see Fig. 23(h)).
此外，自旋螺旋序诱导的极化和∆E<span class="hljs-emphasis">_DD→UU随自旋轨道强度(λ_</span>SOC)线性增加（见图23(h)）。

Thus, spin spiral induced ferroelectricity greatly depends on the spin-orbit coupling.
因此，自旋螺旋诱导的铁电性极大地依赖于自旋轨道耦合。

Further, the polarization and ∆EDD→UU energy can be tuned by varying the interlayer distance (refer to Fig. 23(i)), as discussed previously for other systems.
此外，如前面对其他体系的讨论，极化和∆E<span class="hljs-emphasis">_DD→UU能量可以通过改变层间距来调节（参见图23(i)）。

Similarly, the VI2 trilayer system exhibits sliding ferroelectricity in addition to the spin spiral induced ferroelectricity with ∆EDDD→UUU of 0.22 meV/unit cell between the AAB and ABB configurations (see Fig. 23(j), (k) &amp; (l)).
类似地，VI2三层体系除了自旋螺旋诱导的铁电性外，还表现出滑动铁电性，在AAB和ABB构型之间，∆E_</span>DDD→UUU为0.22 meV/晶胞（见图23(j), (k)和(l)）。

Fig. 23: (a) Top and side view of VI2 monolayer.
图23: (a) VI2单层的俯视图和侧视图。

Noncollinear Y (120°-ordered) AFM order with (b) positive and (c) negative spin spiral chirality in VI2 monolayer.
VI2单层中具有(b) 正和(c) 负自旋螺旋手性的非共线Y（120°有序）反铁磁序。

(d) A schematic demonstrating spin spiral order induced electric polarization.
(d) 展示自旋螺旋序诱导电极化的示意图。

(e) The variation in energy difference between down and up spin spiral chirality (∆ED→U) with the external out-of-plane electric field in VI2 monolayer.
(e) VI2单层中，向下和向上自旋螺旋手性之间的能量差(∆E<span class="hljs-emphasis">_D→U)随外部面外电场的变化。

(f) The energy distribution plot for VI2 bilayer showing different stackings (with reference to AB).
(f) VI2双层显示不同堆垛（以AB为参考）的能量分布图。

(g) The polarization and ∆EDD→UU along the ferroelectric switching pathway AB→AB′.
(g) 沿铁电翻转路径AB→AB′的极化和∆E_</span>DD→UU。

(h) The dependence of polarization on spin-orbit coupling.
(h) 极化对自旋轨道耦合的依赖性。

(i) The variation in polarization and ∆EDD→UU with the interlayer distance.
(i) 极化和∆E<span class="hljs-emphasis">_DD→UU随层间距的变化。

(j) The energy profile for VI2 trilayer showing different stackings.
(j) VI2三层显示不同堆垛的能量分布图。

(k) The energy distribution plot for VI2 trilayer showing different stackings (with reference to AAB).
(k) VI2三层显示不同堆垛（以AAB为参考）的能量分布图。

(l) The polarization and ∆EDDD→UUU along the ferroelectric switching pathway ABB→AAB′.
(l) 沿铁电翻转路径ABB→AAB′的极化和∆E_</span>DDD→UUU。

Reprinted with permission from ref. [100]. Copyright © 2024 American Physical Society.
经许可重印自参考文献[100]。版权所有 © 2024 美国物理学会。

<span class="hljs-section"># 3.7 CrI3/MnSe2 heterobilayer</span>
<span class="hljs-section"># 3.7 CrI3/MnSe2 异质双层</span>

Huang and Kan examined the relationship between electronic polarization and spin interaction through tight-binding and first-principles calculations.[102]
Huang和Kan通过紧束缚模型和第一性原理计算，研究了电子极化与自旋相互作用之间的关系。[102]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Tight-binding**</span>: 紧束缚模型，一种计算固体能带结构的方法，认为电子主要局域在原子周围，通过原子轨道之间的跳跃来传播。

Their findings suggest that in CrI3/MnSe2 and some other vdW magnetic hetero-bilayers, the interlayer magnetic order has a significant impact on the out-of-plane electronic polarization, potentially reversing it entirely.
他们的发现表明，在CrI3/MnSe2和其他一些范德华磁性异质双层中，层间磁序对面外电子极化有显著影响，甚至可能使其完全反转。

They also proposed that interlayer sliding could enable non-volatile switching of the magnetic order, resulting in electric polarization reversal and highlighting the presence of a non-volatile magnetoelectric coupling effect in the CrI3/MnSe2 heterobilayer.
他们还提出，层间滑动可以实现磁序的非易失性翻转，从而导致电极化反转，并突显了CrI3/MnSe2异质双层中存在非易失性磁电耦合效应。

Fig. 24(c) shows the energy profile as a function of interlayer sliding.
图24(c)显示了作为层间滑动函数的能量分布。

Three distinct configurations are identified: S1 is global minimum with FM order, S2 is local minimum with AFM character, while and S3 is global maximum.
确定了三种不同的构型：S1是具有铁磁序的全局最小值，S2是具有反铁磁特征的局部最小值，而S3是全局最大值。

For S1 structure, the PiFM is -0.0073 eÅ/u.c. (indicating a charge transfer from the MnSe2 to CrI3 layer), while the PiAFM is 0.0202 eÅ/u.c.
对于S1结构，P<span class="hljs-emphasis">_i^FM是-0.0073 eÅ/u.c.（表明电荷从MnSe2层转移到CrI3层），而P_</span>i^AFM是0.0202 eÅ/u.c.

The polarization values for the clamped ions case were similar as reported above, thus, suggesting the dominance of electronic over ionic polarization.
固定离子情况下的极化值与上述报告相似，因此表明电子极化占主导地位。

Fig. 24: Top view of atomic structures of (a) CrI3 and (b) MnSe2 monolayers.
图24: (a) CrI3和(b) MnSe2单层原子结构的俯视图。

(c) Energy profiles of CrI3/MnSe2 heterobilayer as a function of interlayer sliding, indicating sliding configurations S1, S2, and S3.
(c) CrI3/MnSe2异质双层能量作为层间滑动函数的分布图，指示了滑动构型S1、S2和S3。

> ### 第一部分：咱们先聊点家常，搞懂这些“洋词儿”是啥意思
> 
> 咱们这篇文章，讲的是个前沿科技，叫**“滑动就能生电，电还能存信息”**的技术。在给您讲文章之前，咱们得先弄明白几个他们年轻人常挂在嘴边的词儿，其实啊，掰开了揉碎了，特简单。
> 
> 1.  **二维材料：** 您见过咱们家墙上贴的年画吧？那是有长、有宽，还有厚度的。但“二维材料”就像一张薄得不能再薄的纸，薄到什么程度呢？薄到只有一层或者几层原子，您可以想象成是只有一个原子那么厚的“超级保鲜膜”。文章里提到的六方氮化硼（h-BN）、二硫化钼（MoS2）就是这种材料。
> 2.  **范德华力（文章里叫vdW）：** 您想啊，这“超级保鲜膜”一层一层地摞在一起，它们之间靠什么粘着呢？不是靠胶水，而是靠一种特别微弱的吸引力，就像您把两张塑料片贴在一起，它们会稍微吸住，但轻轻一搓就能分开。这种微弱的力量，就是范德华力。
> 3.  **铁电性：** 这是咱们这篇文章的核心。您可以把有“铁电性”的材料想象成一个**微小的、可以翻转的“电荷指南针”**。这个指南针，自己就带着一个方向固定的电荷（这叫“自发电极化”），而且，您给它加一个外部的电场，它的电荷方向还能“唰”地一下翻转过来。这一正一反，就像计算机里的“0”和“1”两种状态，可以用来存储信息，而且断电了信息还在，不会忘。
> 4.  **滑动铁电性（Sliding Ferroelectricity）：** 好，这是咱们今天的主角！传统的“铁电性”是通过材料内部原子“移动”来实现电荷翻转的。但“滑动铁电性”更巧妙，它不靠原子自己动，而是**靠两层“超级保鲜膜”之间，像推拉门一样，发生一个极其微小的横向错位（滑动）**。这一滑，材料整体的电荷分布就变了，上面带正电，下面带负电，滑回来，正负电就反过来了。因为层与层之间那点范德华力很弱，所以滑动起来特别省劲儿，速度快，还不容易坏。这就像您用指尖轻轻一推，就把一个开关给拨动了。
>

> ### 第二部分：来，咱们一句一句“翻译”这篇论文
> 
> 有了上面的基础，咱们就开始看这篇论文到底说了些啥。我把它从“洋文”翻译成咱们能听懂的大白话。
> 
> **论文题目：**
> 
> -   **英文：** Recent advances in theoretical investigations of sliding ferroelectricity in layered and van der Waals two-dimensional materials
> -   **白话翻译：** “最近，科学家们在理论上对‘层状和靠范德华力结合的二维材料里的滑动生电现象’研究，又有了哪些新进展。”
> 
> **论文摘要（Abstract）：**
> 
> -   **英文：** 随着对更快、更高效、非易失性存储器技术的需求增长，铁电材料已成为现代电子设备不可或缺的一部分。由范德华层间相对运动引起的滑动铁电性，在层状和二维材料中比传统铁电性更普遍。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 现在我们用的手机、电脑，都希望它运行更快、更省电、存的东西断电了还不丢。能满足这些要求的“铁电材料”就成了宝贝。而“滑动铁电性”这种特殊类型，在那些超薄的材料里特别容易找到，比老式的铁电性更常见。
>     
> -   **英文：** 这篇综述探讨了理论上提出的滑动铁电材料，讨论了其来源于电荷转移和轨道畸变。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 这篇文章啊，就是把科学家们理论上预测出来的、能“滑动生电”的各种材料，归拢起来说一说。顺便也聊聊，为啥一滑动就能生电呢？根本原因在于两层材料之间，电荷发生了微小的转移，而且围绕原子的电子云形状也发生了扭曲（轨道畸变）。
>     
> -   **英文：** 用分子系统取代层，为显著提高存储密度提供了一条有希望的途径。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 科学家们还有个更大胆的想法：如果把一整个滑动的层，换成一个个独立的小分子，那每个分子都能存一个“0”或“1”，存储的信息量就能成千上万倍地增加！
>     
> -   **英文：** 分析了外部刺激（如应变、压力和电场）的影响，以及激光诱导的极化翻转和它与“动态多铁性”的关联。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 文章还聊了，给材料施加点压力、拉力，或者电场，会对“滑动生电”有啥影响。甚至，用激光照一下，也能让电荷翻转，这个过程还能产生一个微小的磁场，这叫“动态多铁性”，听着就挺玄乎的。
>     
> -   **英文：** 多层系统中的跨层滑动铁电性……增加了可能表现出滑动铁电性的材料范围。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 以前以为，只有相邻的两层才能滑动生电。现在发现，我滑第一层，能影响到隔一层的第三层，这叫“隔山打牛式”的滑动生电。这下，能表现出这种特性的材料种类就更多了。
>     
> -   **英文：** 最近提出的莫尔超晶格中的铁电翻转，增加了在器件应用中利用更多滑动铁电性的可能性。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 把两层材料像拧魔方一样，稍微错开一个角度，会形成一种叫“莫尔条纹”的图案。在这种结构里也能实现滑动生电，这为以后的应用打开了新思路。
>     
> -   **英文：** 该综述强调了自旋纹理对铁电极化和层极化的依赖性，这在自旋电子学中具有潜在应用。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 电子除了带电荷，还有一个叫“自旋”的性质，就像一个小陀螺在转。这个“自转”的方向，居然能被“滑动生电”的电荷方向控制！这个特点可以用来做新型的“自旋电子学”器件，速度更快，功耗更低。
>     
> -   **英文：** 铁性序的耦合……提供了多态翻转的机会和通过操纵一种性质来调节另一种性质的能力。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 这种材料有时是“多面手”，它的“滑动生电”特性，还能和“磁性”、“弹性”这些特性搅和在一起，互相影响。我改变它的电荷，它的磁性就变了；我拉它一下，它的电荷也可能变了。这就能实现“一石二鸟”甚至“一石多鸟”的奇妙控制。

> -   **英文：** 最后，连续介质机电学和热力学分析揭示了一级铁电-顺电相变。尽管其能量翻转势垒很低，但滑动铁电极化的鲁棒性归因于二维材料较高的层内刚度。
>     
> -   **白话翻译：** 最后，文章用理论模型分析了，这种“滑动生电”状态是怎么随着温度升高而消失的（这叫相变）。有意思的是，虽然翻转电荷需要的能量特别小，意味着很容易翻，但这种状态本身却非常稳定、皮实（鲁棒性）。为啥呢？因为虽然层与层之间很滑溜，但每一层本身，就像一张结实的纸，要把它整张撕开或揉皱却很难，这种“层内刚度”保证了整体结构的稳定性。

> ### 第三部分：太奶，我给您把这篇论文的“筋骨”抽出来，总结一遍
> 
> 您看，内容就是上面那些。接下来，我按照您的要求，把这篇论文的“骨架”给您拎清楚。
> 
> -   **论文题目：** 《层状和范德华二维材料中滑动铁电性的理论研究最新进展》
>     
> -   **摘要：** 就像咱们上面翻译的那样，这篇文章主要综述了“滑动铁电性”这个现象。它是什么，为什么会产生，都发生在哪些材料里，有哪些外界的因素能影响它，它还能和材料的磁性、弹性等其他性质搞“跨界合作”，最后还讲了它为啥既容易翻转又非常稳定。总之，它描绘了一个未来能做出更快、更省电、存得更多的电子器件的蓝图。
>     
> -   **研究问题：** 这篇文章不是解决一个具体问题，而是回答了一个大问题：“‘滑动铁电性’这个新兴领域，发展得怎么样了？” 它要梳理清楚，从理论上，人们已经知道了哪些材料有这本事，这本事是怎么来的，以及怎么利用和调控这个本事。
>     
> -   **方法：** 这篇文章不是自己做实验，而是个大“综述”（Review）。它把全世界科学家在这个领域发表的、用计算机做理论计算（第一性原理计算）的论文，像珠子一样串起来，进行归纳、比较和评述。它总结的方法，都是别人用过的理论方法，比如密度泛函理论（DFT）、微动弹性带（NEB）方法计算能量壁垒等。
>     
> -   **结论：**
>     
>     1.  **来源：** 滑动铁电性广泛存在，源于层间电荷转移和电子云扭曲。
>     2.  **调控：** 可以用压力、电场、光照等多种手段来调控它的电荷翻转。
>     3.  **材料：** 发现了从简单的氮化硼（BN）到复杂的石墨烯、过渡金属硫化物等一大类材料都有这特性，甚至可以通过“隔层滑动”和“分子替换”来拓展范围。
>     4.  **耦合：** 它能和磁性、弹性等性质耦合，实现多功能调控，这在自旋电子学和多态存储上很有用。
>     5.  **稳定性：** 虽然翻转能耗低，但凭借单层材料本身的“硬骨头”（高刚度），它的电荷状态能保持得非常好。
>     6.  **应用：** 在隧道结、存储器等方面有巨大应用潜力。
> -   **创新点（这篇文章的亮点）：**
>     
>     1.  **强调理论视角：** 它不是泛泛而谈，而是专门从“理论计算”这个角度来总结，把各种计算预测的材料和机制梳理得清清楚楚。
>     2.  **系统梳理了调控手段：** 把应变、电场、激光等外部刺激对滑动铁电性的影响讲得很细致。
>     3.  **突出了前沿交叉：** 浓墨重彩地讲了“滑动铁电性”与“磁性”（多铁性）、“拓扑性质”和“自旋电子学”的交叉，这是当前最热的方向。
>     4.  **提出了新概念：** 总结了像“跨层滑动铁电性”、“莫尔滑动铁电性”和“动态多铁性”这些新潮概念，拓宽了视野。
> -   **不足之处：**
>     
>     1.  **缺乏实验验证的讨论：** 它主要讲理论预测，但很多理论说得天花乱坠的材料，在实验室里制备出来可能非常困难，或者性质没那么好。文章对理论和实验之间的差距着墨不多。
>     2.  **对实际应用的挑战过于乐观：** 虽然提到了应用前景，但要把这些微小的、需要靠滑动整层原子来工作的器件，做到成千上万个集成在一个芯片上，还要保证它们次次都听话，难度极大。文章对这方面工程化的挑战讨论较少。
>     3.  **专业门槛高，总结性太强：** 对想要了解具体某个材料计算细节的科研人员来说，它可能不够深入；对您这样想了解个大概的读者，它又太晦涩。不过，这后一点，在咱们这番对话之后，已经不成问题啦！
